MEASURED on GX010142 rally #3 (21-25s): the *tracked* shuttle is 94% adjacent-court (x-norm ~0.88), BUT the raw candidates ALSO carry a **prominent-court shuttle in 83% of the window's frames** (x-norm ~0.56), **both shuttles present in 76%**, and a greedy-NN reconstruction yields a **continuous prominent-court rally track** (75% coverage, 431px vertical excursion, 8.6px median per-frame jump, ~2.4s sustained). **So the prominent rally the owner saw IS in the data** — recoverable by **multi-track selection over the already-cached candidates (zero GPU)**, just not by gate-tuning the single track. → reframes the whole thread: the ceiling is **track SELECTION, not the eyes**. **NEXT DESIGN THREAD: multi-track + court-aware selection** (a window is valid if ANY sustained track lives in the prominent court) — this is **recall-positive** (recovers the prominent rally, a G1 miss) AND precision-positive (drops the adjacent one), unlike the current subtractive single-track gate. It also subsumes the **ghost** (a stuck-pixel track has ~0 motion energy → never selected). Repros: `scratch/analyze_multishuttle_142.py` + `scratch/reconstruct_prominent_track_142.py` + `scratch/overlay_tracks_142.py` (trajectory overlay) + `scratch/extract_multishuttle_frames_142.py`. ✅ **VISUALLY CONFIRMED (2026-06-19, `artifacts/multishuttle_142/tracks_overlay_crop.png`):** the green prominent track is a textbook **rally arc anchored to a player mid-stroke** (bottom-left) → second player (center) — a real foreground-court rally; the yellow tracker output is a tangle over the **far-right adjacent court** (player at right margin). Two shuttles, two courts, detector saw both. ⚠ Corrected mid-investigation: the adjacent track is NOT a static fixture (528px y-span — a real moving adjacent rally). ⚠ Still n=1 clip; 31% multi-candidate includes detector noise (selection scoring must disambiguate). Owner collecting more two-shuttle examples; **DO NOT PR yet** (owner's call — this is design).
50	- **OTHER OPEN THREADS:** (C6) **player-anchored polygon auto-derivation** — person features ALREADY extracted on GX010142 → `output/person_GX010142.json` (`scratch/extract_person_19june.py`; median 3 players/frame). Raw player COUNT doesn't separate rally #1 (need court-LOCALIZED occupancy via `fusion_features.in_court_counts` + the same polygon — players inside the prominent court ≈ 0 during rally #1). Foot-points can auto-derive the polygon. (Analysis DONE) **multicourt quality workflow measured the gate vs golden on the 4 multicourt clips → `output/multicourt_analysis.md`.** Honest result: the gate is a **REAL precision win ONLY where a genuinely separable adjacent court exists** — **Boxhill_Doubles ΔP +0.096 / ΔF1 +0.078, recall FLAT, 20 adjacent-court FPs dropped (all median x>0.80)**. NULL/marginal elsewhere: mahadevpura_2 +0.025 (trims 4 edge FPs, no separable court), **GX010128 + Badminton_BXH_2 = exact NULL** (their over-fire is on-prominent-court over-merge/boundary FPs the court gate can't touch, NOT adjacent-court rallies). 🔑 **2nd detection-layer finding (besides single-shuttle): STUCK-TRACKER artifacts** — GX010128 has 284 pts at exactly x=1680px, BXH_2 ~41% of pts parked at court-center — a fixed-pixel WASB false-lock (a data-quality bug, not a court issue). ⚠ NO Gemini preds for these → "closeness to Gemini" can't be direct; Gemini-on-multicourt run = a follow-up (alias degraded today). **Verdict: the prominent-court gate is a worthwhile precision lever for TRUE multicourt (separable adjacent court), not a cure-all for multicourt over-fire (which has 3 distinct causes: adjacent-court rallies [gate fixes], on-court over-merge [other levers], stuck-tracker [detection fix]).**

1	# Deployment report — first real GPU run on GCP (L4, asia-south1), 2026-06-20
2	
3	**What this is.** The first end-to-end **non-mock** run of the decoupled pipeline on a rented cloud GPU:
4	the M3.5 native WASB runner extracting REAL shuttle trajectories on a GCP **NVIDIA L4**, driving the
5	bucket-driven `worker infer` / `worker train`. Closes M1/M2/M3.5 of `04-BUILD-AND-TEST-PLAN.md` on cloud
6	hardware (per ADR-011 scope). Written for posterity + to make the next deploy one-shot.
7	
8	## TL;DR
9	- ✅ **Decoupling works + the GPU is genuinely used.** `worker infer detector_impl=native` ran the full
10	  pipeline on the L4 (gcs:// in → real WASB extraction on GPU → rallies → gcs:// out): **22 rallies** on
11	  the 180 s smoke clip.
12	- ✅ **M3.5 native runner runs on a real cloud GPU** (NVIDIA L4 23 GB) — same `wasb_infer.py`, device cuda.
13	- 📊 **Cross-GPU parity is NOT byte-identical** (L4 vs the 2070): 684/1195 frames differ by **sub-pixel**
14	  amounts (cuDNN across architectures) + a few visibility flips → the M3.5 gate belongs at the
15	  **rally/window level**, not CSV bytes. (Same-GPU IS byte-exact: the 2070 gave 1194/1195.)
16	- ⏳ **M2 real-trajectory `worker train`** validated in-flight (16k+ windows extracted on the L4); full
17	  weights bundle is a next-session item (env preserved / one-shot re-port).
18	- 🎬 **Highlight reel** of the M1 rallies pushed → `outputs/05e0e577…/highlight_smoke_l4.mp4` (22 rallies,
19	  94 s). **Smoke-tested:** valid h264 1080p, 95.2 s, **clean full decode (`ffmpeg -f null`, exit 0)**, and a
20	  5-frame contact sheet **visually confirms real badminton rallies** (court, net, players mid-rally).
21	- ⛔ **DigitalOcean GPU is blocked at the provider** — token valid, but the account has **0 GPU Droplet
22	  sizes** available (needs GPU-Droplet enablement in the DO console). So training-on-DO-credits is gated
23	  until that's enabled; this run used GCP.
24	
25	## Resources provisioned (project `gen-lang-client-0306026826`, billing "Khelsutra Billing")
26	| Resource | Identifier | Console |
27	|---|---|---|
28	| GCS bucket | `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus` (asia-south1) | storage/browser |
29	| Service account | `rally-worker@…` — storage.objectAdmin **+ monitoring.metricWriter + logging.logWriter** (added for Ops Agent) | iam-admin/serviceaccounts |
30	| Budget | `rally-100usd-guard` ($100, 50/90/100%) | billing budgets |
31	| GPU quota | `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` → **1** (granted after a Cloud Quotas API request) | iam-admin/quotas |
32	| GPU VM | `rally-gpu` — **L4 / g2-standard-4 / asia-south1-c**, image `common-cu129-ubuntu-2204-nvidia-580` | compute/instances |
33	| Ops Agent | installed on the VM → GPU/NVML metrics + logs in Observability | VM → Observability |
34	
35	Bucket layout staged: `models/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar`, `labels/*.rallies.csv` (7), `videos/`
36	(smoke + the 2 smallest corpus videos), `outputs/`, `weights/`.
37	
38	## Run results
39	- **M3.5 parity** (`wasb_infer.py --device cuda`, smoke clip, limit 1200): produced
40	  `outputs/parity/native_smoke_l4.csv` (1200 frames). vs the 2070 WSL baseline: **511/1195 identical,
41	  684 differ sub-pixel** (e.g. 968.01→967.87 px), visible 682 vs 684. → parity within ~tenths-of-a-px
42	  cross-GPU; **not** byte-exact across GPUs.
43	- **M1 decoupled infer** (`worker infer detector_impl=native`, smoke clip, full 5393 windows on GPU):
44	  **22 rallies** → `outputs/<md5>/intervals.csv` + `report.json` pushed.
45	- **M2 real-trajectory train** (`worker train --trajectory-source detector`, mahadevpura-1 + GX030094):
46	  **real extraction VALIDATED in-flight** — the native runner extracted **16 000+/21 195 windows of
47	  GX030094 on the L4 GPU** before the clean-slate teardown (overwhelming evidence the real-trajectory
48	  train path works e2e). The full train → versioned weights (`weights/0.1.0-l4/`) is a **next-session
49	  item**: re-port is one-shot via the fixed `gpu_setup.sh`; **set `indexing.wasb.stream_video=false`
50	  for the long training videos** (disk extraction keeps the GPU fed — finding #6).
51	
52	## Findings / gotchas (all fixed or noted)
53	1. **conda Terms-of-Service gate** — modern conda blocks non-interactive `conda create`
54	   (`CondaToSNonInteractiveError`). **Fixed in `gpu_setup.sh`** (`conda tos accept …`).
55	2. **WASB-SBDT `requirements.txt` is incomplete** — `src/` imports `hydra`, `pandas`, etc. it omits.
56	   **Fixed in `gpu_setup.sh`** (explicit `hydra-core omegaconf pandas opencv-python-headless pillow scipy
57	   tqdm pyyaml matplotlib numpy<1.24`).
58	3. **T4 capacity stockout** in `asia-south1-a` → **L4 in `-c`** landed (g2-standard-4; L4 is built into g2,
59	   no `--accelerator` flag). Try L4/other zones when T4 is unavailable.
60	4. **Spot does NOT bypass `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS`** (tested) — both on-demand + Spot need the quota.
61	5. **SSH-foreground long commands die on a dropped connection** — run them `nohup`'d on the box + poll the
62	   log. (Runbook updated.)
63	6. **Streaming vs disk for extraction** — streaming (the native default) is the win for SHORT clips (skips
64	   the 46k-PNG write), but for a LONG training video its single-process decode **starves the GPU**; the
65	   disk path (`stream_video=false`, parallel `num_workers`) keeps the GPU fed → **prefer disk extraction
66	   for long training videos**. (Follow-up: a per-path default / train-uses-disk knob.)
67	7. **Observability** needs THREE things, all initially missing on the bare Gemini project: (a) the
68	   **Cloud Logging + Monitoring APIs ENABLED** (`gcloud services enable logging… monitoring…`) — this was
69	   the actual empty-graphs cause: the agent installs + runs but its exports get
70	   `PermissionDenied/SERVICE_DISABLED` until the APIs are on; (b) the SA's **monitoring.metricWriter +
71	   logging.logWriter** roles; (c) the **Ops Agent** itself (direct install — the console OS-policy install
72	   is blocked on DLVM images; that console error is a red herring). All done + the agent restarted →
73	   exports clean (0 errors). Runbook §3b has the full sequence (best baked into the VM startup-script).
74	
75	## Cost
76	L4 (g2-standard-4) on-demand ≈ **$0.70–0.85/hr**; the validation run is ~1–1.5 hr ⇒ **~$1**. The `$100`
77	budget alert is the backstop. **STOP/DELETE the VM when idle** — `deploy/gcp/teardown.sh`.
78	
79	## Teardown
80	`bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh delete` (frees the GPU; keeps the bucket). The bucket/SA/budget are retained
81	for the GCP serving path (ADR-011).
82	
83	**Session-end state (2026-06-20):** the **L4 VM was DELETED** (clean slate, **$0 ongoing compute**) — the
84	bucket, SA (`rally-worker`), and `$100` budget are retained. The next session **re-ports one-shot** via the
85	fixed `gpu_setup.sh` (`deploy/gcp/README.md`) — re-porting is itself the repro. A leaked DO-token file
86	(`dop_v1_…`, accidentally written as a filename) was removed from the repo root → **rotate that DO token**.
87	
88	## Next steps
89	- Stage the **full corpus** for real training via the Drive folder
90	  (`1sHGL7iacnmM80ChT-p2cKy638gK504T_`, gdown-able) → bucket `videos/`; raw GoPro source at
91	  `F:\[Khelsutra] GoPro Hero Black 12`.
92	- For real training, extract long videos via the **disk path** (faster, GPU-fed).
93	- **DO path:** enable GPU Droplets on the DO account → then training can run on DO credits (ADR-011).
94	- The `gpu_setup.sh` fixes here make the next port one-shot.

1	# GCP provisioning runbook — DECOUPLED_COMPUTE GPU + storage (asia-south1)
2	
3	Co-located **GPU VM + GCS bucket in ONE region** (asia-south1 / Mumbai) for the first real
4	(non-mock) WASB training + inference. This is the **DO→GCP pivot** (ADR-010): DigitalOcean GPU
5	Droplets are North-America-only (no Singapore/India GPU), so we moved GPU + storage to GCP, which
6	has GPUs in asia-south1 (Mumbai) and asia-southeast1 (Singapore). Owner picked **Mumbai** (closest
7	to Bangalore → lowest latency).
8	
9	> **Cost discipline (read first):** a running GPU VM is the only meaningful cost. Use the cheapest
10	> adequate GPU (**T4**), **STOP or DELETE the VM the moment a run finishes** (a stopped VM bills only
11	> for its disk), and keep the **$100 budget alert** live. `teardown.sh` switches everything off.
12	
13	---
14	
15	## 0. Live resources (created 2026-06-20 — the "switch-off" inventory)
16	
17	| Resource | Identifier | Cost when idle | How to switch off |
18	|---|---|---|---|
19	| Project | `gen-lang-client-0306026826` (billing **Khelsutra Billing** `01420D-419EE0-53D83C`) | — | n/a (shared Gemini project) |
20	| GCS bucket | `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus` (asia-south1, UBLA) | ~$0.02/GB/mo storage | `gcloud storage rm -r` (⚠ data loss) |
21	| Service account | `rally-worker@gen-lang-client-0306026826.iam.gserviceaccount.com` (`roles/storage.objectAdmin`) | $0 | `gcloud iam service-accounts delete` |
22	| SA key | `~/.gcp/rally-worker-sa.json` (local only, **never** committed) | $0 | delete the file + the key |
23	| Budget alert | `rally-100usd-guard` — $100, alerts at 50/90/100% | $0 | `gcloud billing budgets delete` |
24	| **GPU VM** | **NOT created** — blocked on `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` quota (see §2) | **~$0.35/hr (T4) running, ~$0.01/hr stopped** | `teardown.sh` (stop or delete) |
25	| APIs enabled | serviceusage, compute, cloudbilling, billingbudgets, iam, cloudresourcemanager | $0 | leave on (free) |
26	
27	Run `bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh` (see §6) to stop/delete the billable bits between sessions.
28	
29	---
30	
31	## 1. One-time project bootstrap (owner, console — already done 2026-06-20)
32	
33	A bare Gemini/AI-Studio project has the **Service Usage API disabled**, which blocks *all* gcloud
34	provisioning (gcloud's own `services enable` needs that API → chicken-and-egg). These were enabled
35	in the console; recorded here so a fresh project can be bootstrapped the same way:
36	
37	1. **Service Usage API** — https://console.developers.google.com/apis/api/serviceusage.googleapis.com/overview?project=gen-lang-client-0306026826
38	2. **Link a billing account** (a payment method) — https://console.cloud.google.com/billing/linkedaccount?project=gen-lang-client-0306026826
39	3. After (1), the rest enable via gcloud (done): `gcloud services enable compute.googleapis.com cloudbilling.googleapis.com billingbudgets.googleapis.com iam.googleapis.com cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com`
40	
41	`provision.sh` (§5) is idempotent and re-runs all of step (3) + the bucket/SA/budget.
42	
43	---
44	
45	## 2. ⛔ GPU quota — the one remaining owner action
46	
47	New GCP projects start at **`GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` = 0**, the *global* cap that gates GPU creation even
48	when a regional per-type quota is non-zero. Confirmed 2026-06-20:
49	
50	```
51	asia-south1  NVIDIA_T4_GPUS = 1   NVIDIA_L4_GPUS = 1   (regional: OK)
52	GLOBAL       GPUS_ALL_REGIONS = 0  ← BLOCKS the VM
53	```
54	
55	**Owner:** IAM & Admin → **Quotas** → filter **"GPUs (all regions)"** → Edit → request **1** → submit.
56	(For a single T4 this is usually approved in minutes; sometimes a short review.) Re-check:
57	
58	```bash
59	gcloud compute project-info describe --project gen-lang-client-0306026826 \
60	  --flatten="quotas[]" --format="table(quotas.metric,quotas.limit)" | grep GPUS_ALL_REGIONS
61	```
62	
63	When that reads `1`, run §3.
64	
65	> **⚠ Spot does NOT skip the quota.** TESTED 2026-06-20: a **Spot** T4 create also fails with
66	> `Quota 'GPUS_ALL_REGIONS' exceeded. Limit: 0.0 globally` — `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` gates **both**
67	> on-demand AND preemptible GPUs, so the increase above is required either way. (A pending request
68	> was filed via the Cloud Quotas API: `POST …/quotaPreferences` `GPUS-ALL-REGIONS-per-project`
69	> → 1.) **Note (2026-06-20 owner decision, ADR-011): GCP is the SERVING path; TRAINING runs on
70	> DigitalOcean (DO credits) — see `SETUP_NEW_MACHINE.md` §2.** Spot is still cheaper once the quota
71	> is granted, and the resumable cache makes preemption recoverable.
72	
73	---
74	
75	## 3. Create the GPU VM (after quota ≥ 1)
76	
77	T4 is plenty for WASB + Gen-0. A local SSD gives a fast `/scratch`. *(The native runner defaults to
78	**streaming** — it decodes frames in-process, skipping the slow cv2 PNG extraction; bit-identical to disk,
79	verified on a real GPU 2026-06-20. Set `indexing.wasb.stream_video=false` only if a container/codec cv2
80	can't seek; `WASB_SCRATCH` still backs the disk path.)*
81	
82	```bash
83	gcloud compute instances create rally-gpu \
84	  --project=gen-lang-client-0306026826 \
85	  --zone=asia-south1-a \
86	  --machine-type=n1-standard-8 \
87	  --accelerator=type=nvidia-tesla-t4,count=1 \
88	  --maintenance-policy=TERMINATE --provisioning-model=STANDARD \
89	  --image-family=ubuntu-2404-lts-amd64 --image-project=ubuntu-os-cloud \
90	  --boot-disk-size=120GB --boot-disk-type=pd-balanced \
91	  --local-ssd=interface=NVME \
92	  --service-account=rally-worker@gen-lang-client-0306026826.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
93	  --scopes=cloud-platform
94	```
95	
96	**Spot variant (cheaper; still needs the §2 quota — Spot is NOT a quota bypass):** same command with
97	`--provisioning-model=SPOT` (replacing `STANDARD`). Add `--instance-termination-action=STOP` to keep
98	the disk on preemption so a re-run resumes. Cheaper, can be preempted.
99	
100	> **Validated image (2026-06-20):** `--image-family=common-cu129-ubuntu-2204-nvidia-580
101	> --image-project=deeplearning-platform-release` ships the NVIDIA driver (no install-on-boot wait;
102	> `nvidia-smi` works immediately) — used for the first real L4 run. `gpu_setup.sh` installs miniconda
103	> on it. **T4 was capacity-stocked-out in `asia-south1-a`; an L4 (`g2-standard-4`, no `--accelerator`
104	> flag — L4 is built into g2) in `asia-south1-c` landed** — try L4/other zones if T4 is unavailable.
105	
106	### 3b. Observability — Ops Agent (GPU graphs + logs in the console)
107	The VM **Observability → GPU** graphs + Logs need the **Ops Agent** (the console's OS-policy install is
108	blocked on DLVM images). Install it directly + grant the worker SA the write roles (it otherwise only has
109	storage), then GPU/NVML metrics + syslog flow to Cloud Monitoring/Logging (tab populates in ~2-3 min):
110	```bash
111	P=gen-lang-client-0306026826; SA=rally-worker@$P.iam.gserviceaccount.com
112	# 1. ENABLE the APIs (bare Gemini project has them OFF — the agent installs but its exports get
113	#    PermissionDenied/SERVICE_DISABLED until these are on; THIS was the empty-graphs cause 2026-06-20):
114	gcloud services enable logging.googleapis.com monitoring.googleapis.com --project $P
115	# 2. let rally-worker publish metrics + logs:
116	gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding $P --member="serviceAccount:$SA" --role=roles/monitoring.metricWriter --condition=None
117	gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding $P --member="serviceAccount:$SA" --role=roles/logging.logWriter   --condition=None
118	# 3. on the box (created with --scopes=cloud-platform):
119	curl -sSO https://dl.google.com/cloudagents/add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh && sudo bash add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh --also-install
120	# (if the agent was installed BEFORE the APIs were enabled: `sudo systemctl restart google-cloud-ops-agent`)
121	```
122	> Best: bake `curl …add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh|bash --also-install` into the VM
123	> `--metadata=startup-script` so observability is automatic on every box.
124	
125	> **Long remote steps must be detached.** A plain `ssh … "<long cmd>"` dies if the SSH drops (seen
126	> mid-build, 2026-06-20) — run them `nohup`'d on the box (`nohup bash setup.sh >log 2>&1 &`) and poll
127	> the log, so a dropped connection can't kill the install.
128	
129	> If `asia-south1-a` reports no T4 capacity, try `-b` / `-c`. The Ubuntu image needs the NVIDIA
130	> driver: either add `--metadata=install-nvidia-driver=True` style startup, or after SSH run GCP's
131	> driver installer, **or** use a Deep-Learning-VM image (`--image-family=common-cu124-ubuntu-2204`
132	> `--image-project=deeplearning-platform-release`) which ships the driver. Confirm `nvidia-smi` works
133	> before §4.
134	
135	SSH: `gcloud compute ssh rally-gpu --zone=asia-south1-a`.
136	
137	---
138	
139	## 4. Port the repo + WASB env onto the box
140	
141	The `deploy/digitalocean/` scripts are **provider-agnostic** (they detect the local NVMe / install
142	torch); reuse them on GCP:
143	
144	```bash
145	# from your machine — ship the repo:
146	git archive --format=tar.gz -o /tmp/rally.tgz HEAD
147	gcloud compute scp /tmp/rally.tgz rally-gpu:/root/ --zone=asia-south1-a
148	gcloud compute ssh rally-gpu --zone=asia-south1-a -- 'mkdir -p ~/rally && tar -xzf ~/rally.tgz -C ~/rally'
149	
150	# on the box:
151	cd ~/rally
152	sudo bash deploy/digitalocean/mount_scratch.sh && export TMPDIR=/scratch   # detects the local NVMe
153	./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh --gpu                                   # venv + torch cu124 (confirm cuda True)
154	. .venv/bin/activate && pip install -e .[storage-gcs]                      # GCS backend
155	bash deploy/digitalocean/gpu_setup.sh                                      # native `wasb` conda env + WASB-SBDT
156	
157	# secrets (NOT in repo): the GCS SA JSON + a rotated Gemini key
158	gcloud compute scp ~/.gcp/rally-worker-sa.json rally-gpu:/root/.gcp/sa.json --zone=asia-south1-a
159	# on the box: export GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=/root/.gcp/sa.json
160	mkdir -p /root/.keys && printf '%s' '<ROTATED_GEMINI_KEY>' > /root/.keys/GEMINI_API_KEY && chmod 600 /root/.keys/GEMINI_API_KEY
161	```
162	
163	WASB weights (`wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar`) must be present at
164	`~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/` (gpu_setup.sh does NOT fetch them — stage from Drive/bucket).
165	Point the native runner at them: `export WASB_WEIGHTS=~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar`
166	and `export WASB_PYTHON=$(conda run -n wasb which python)` (or the env's python path). See
167	`docs/TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md` for the WASB env. **WASB-C3:** weight provenance is an open legal item
168	before sharing any trajectories/model externally — internal extraction + Gen-0 is fine.
169	
170	---
171	
172	## 5. Stage the corpus + run the first REAL train/infer
173	
174	Config for GCS (no secrets in config — auth via `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`):
175	
176	```json
177	{ "sport": "badminton",
178	  "indexing": { "default_segmenter": "wasb_hybrid", "detector_impl": "native" },
179	  "storage": { "backend": "local", "gcs": { "project": "gen-lang-client-0306026826" } } }
180	```
181	
182	```bash
183	# stage the 7 Done videos + 7 labels into the bucket (from the co-located box — fast):
184	#   videos -> gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/   labels -> gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/labels/
185	# (owner handles the actual copy; see "Upload options" below)
186	
187	# FIRST REAL training (real WASB trajectories, native runner):
188	RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=native python -m backend.worker train \
189	  --corpus-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus --out-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus \
190	  --version 0.1.0 --trajectory-source detector --segmenter wasb_hybrid --config config.json
191	
192	# FIRST REAL inference on the held-out Video 1 (GX010135_proxy.mp4, no labels):
193	python -m backend.worker infer \
194	  --video-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/GX010135_proxy.mp4 \
195	  --out-uri  gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus --segmenter wasb_hybrid --config config.json
196	```
197	
198	### Upload options (how to get a video in + run inference)
199	The worker resolves `--video-uri` via the storage layer — pick whichever fits:
200	- **GCS bucket (recommended, co-located):** `gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/clip.mp4`. Upload with
201	  `gcloud storage cp clip.mp4 gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/`.
202	- **S3 / DO Spaces / R2:** `s3://bucket/clip.mp4` (install `.[storage-s3]`, creds in `~/.aws/credentials`).
203	- **Local upload:** scp the file to the box and pass a bare path / `local:///root/clip.mp4` (identity passthrough, no copy).
204	- **Google Drive (assumes Drive is MOUNTED):** mount Google Drive for Desktop — a fair requirement to
205	  use Drive files locally — and reference them with `gdrive://<path-under-My-Drive>`. The backend resolves
206	  it against your local mount (auto-detected: Windows `G:\My Drive`, macOS
207	  `~/Library/CloudStorage/GoogleDrive-<acct>/My Drive`; override with `GDRIVE_MOUNT_ROOT` or
208	  `storage.gdrive.mount_root`), then it's plain local I/O — **no service account, no API, no extra install.**
209	  E.g. the golden-corpus folder `…/drive/folders/1sHGL7iacnmM80ChT-p2cKy638gK504T_` mounts at
210	  `G:\My Drive\Sharing\Annotation Videos\Golden Label Videos Request - June 15\`, so
211	  `gdrive://Sharing/Annotation Videos/Golden Label Videos Request - June 15/[Done] Video 4 - Badminton/GX020094_proxy.mp4`
212	  (or just the raw `G:\My Drive\…` path — the `local` backend handles that too). ⚠ A **headless GPU VM has
213	  no mount** — there, stage Drive→bucket and read `gcs://`, or `gdown <file-id> -O clip.mp4` to a local path.
214	
215	---
216	
217	## 6. Switch off between sessions (cost control)
218	
219	```bash
220	bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh stop     # stop the VM (keep disk; ~$0 compute) — fastest resume
221	bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh delete   # delete the VM (no disk cost; full re-port to resume)
222	bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh all      # delete VM + SA + budget (keeps the bucket/data)
223	```
224	
225	The **bucket is never auto-deleted** (it holds the corpus). Delete it explicitly only when done:
226	`gcloud storage rm -r gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus`.
227

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	#
3	# Bootstrap a DigitalOcean / Paperspace Ubuntu box to run the rally pipeline.
4	#
5	#   ./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh           # CPU box (Droplet)            — M0 / M1-cpu
6	#   ./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh --gpu      # GPU box (Paperspace / GPU Droplet) — M1 / M3.5
7	#
8	# Mirrors .github/workflows/ci.yml (Ubuntu) so the box matches CI exactly.
9	# STORAGE-FREE + SECRET-FREE: proves the pipeline runs on the box via the mocked
10	# test suite. No bucket, no Gemini key, no WSL required. See ../../docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/.
11	set -euo pipefail
12	
13	GPU=0
14	[ "${1:-}" = "--gpu" ] && GPU=1
15	
16	echo ">> [1/4] System libraries (ffmpeg + opencv runtime)"
17	sudo apt-get update
18	sudo apt-get install -y python3 python3-venv python3-pip git ffmpeg libgl1
19	# opencv needs libglib2.0-0; the package name differs across Ubuntu releases (t64 on 24.04).
20	sudo apt-get install -y libglib2.0-0t64 || sudo apt-get install -y libglib2.0-0
21	
22	echo ">> [2/4] Python venv (.venv)"
23	python3 -m venv .venv
24	# shellcheck disable=SC1091
25	. .venv/bin/activate
26	python -m pip install --upgrade pip
27	
28	echo ">> [3/4] torch ($([ "$GPU" = 1 ] && echo CUDA || echo CPU)) + package (editable, dev tooling)"
29	if [ "$GPU" = 1 ]; then
30	  # CUDA 12.4 wheels. NOTE: verify your GPU arch is supported by this build.
31	  # The Paperspace free M4000 is Maxwell (sm_52) — old archs are sometimes dropped;
32	  # if `torch.cuda.is_available()` is False on an M4000, use a P4000/RTX4000 or pin
33	  # an older torch. (docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/03 + 06.)
34	  pip install torch torchvision --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124
35	else
36	  pip install torch torchvision --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
37	fi
38	# Runtime deps + pytest/ruff/mypy. The 'reporting' extra (private obsreport over SSH)
39	# is intentionally omitted — it is a soft dep and its tests skip when absent.
40	pip install -e .[dev]
41	
42	echo ">> [4/4] Device check"
43	python - <<'PY'
44	import torch
45	ok = torch.cuda.is_available()
46	print("torch", torch.__version__, "| cuda_available", ok,
47	      "| device", (torch.cuda.get_device_name(0) if ok else "CPU only"))
48	PY
49	
50	cat <<'EOF'
51	
52	>> Bootstrap complete. Run the storage-free, secret-free smoke (mocked suite):
53	
54	     . .venv/bin/activate && python -m pytest -q
55	
56	   Expect ~900 passed. That proves the whole pipeline runs on this box with no GPU,
57	   no WSL, no bucket and no API key. (On a GPU box the device check above should
58	   print cuda_available True.)
59	EOF
60

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	#
3	# Native (no-WSL) WASB shuttle-detector setup for a Linux GPU box — the M3.5 enabler for
4	# real trajectory extraction. Replicates docs/TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md's WSL conda env
5	# *natively* on Linux. Run AFTER ./bootstrap.sh --gpu (which provides CUDA torch for the
6	# main venv); this builds a SEPARATE conda env for WASB (torch 1.11) — never co-installed
7	# with the indexer's torch (framework conflict).
8	#
9	# Requires an NVIDIA GPU + driver (the DO `gpu-*-base` image ships it).
10	#
11	# ⚠ WASB-C3: the pretrained badminton weights' license / source-dataset provenance is NOT
12	# verified here. Fine for internal extraction + Gen-0 bootstrapping; clear it before sharing
13	# any derived trajectories or model. See docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/06.
14	set -euo pipefail
15	
16	MODELS_DIR="${RALLY_MODELS_DIR:-$HOME/models}"
17	CONDA_DIR="${CONDA_DIR:-$HOME/miniconda3}"
18	WASB_REPO="$MODELS_DIR/WASB-SBDT"
19	mkdir -p "$MODELS_DIR"
20	
21	echo "[gpu] [1/5] miniconda (native Linux)"
22	if [ ! -x "$CONDA_DIR/bin/conda" ]; then
23	  curl -sSL https://repo.anaconda.com/miniconda/Miniconda3-latest-Linux-x86_64.sh -o /tmp/mc.sh
24	  bash /tmp/mc.sh -b -p "$CONDA_DIR" && rm -f /tmp/mc.sh
25	fi
26	# shellcheck disable=SC1091
27	source "$CONDA_DIR/etc/profile.d/conda.sh"
28	
29	# Modern conda BLOCKS non-interactive `conda create` until the defaults-channel Terms of Service
30	# are accepted (CondaToSNonInteractiveError). Accept them so the env build doesn't fail on a fresh
31	# box. (Observed on a GCP Deep-Learning-VM image, 2026-06-20.)
32	conda tos accept --override-channels --channel https://repo.anaconda.com/pkgs/main >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
33	conda tos accept --override-channels --channel https://repo.anaconda.com/pkgs/r    >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
34	
35	echo "[gpu] [2/5] WASB-SBDT repo"
36	if [ ! -d "$WASB_REPO/.git" ]; then
37	  git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/nttcom/WASB-SBDT.git "$WASB_REPO"
38	fi
39	
40	echo "[gpu] [3/5] conda env 'wasb' (py3.8 + torch 1.11.0+cu113 — the verified WASB stack)"
41	conda env list | grep -q '/envs/wasb$' || conda create -y -q -n wasb python=3.8
42	conda activate wasb
43	python -m pip install -q torch==1.11.0+cu113 torchvision==0.12.0+cu113 \
44	  --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu113
45	[ -f "$WASB_REPO/requirements.txt" ] && python -m pip install -q -r "$WASB_REPO/requirements.txt" || true
46	# WASB-SBDT's requirements.txt is INCOMPLETE: src/ imports hydra, pandas, etc. that it omits, so
47	# `import wasb_infer` deps fail without these. (Found on the GCP L4 port, 2026-06-20.) numpy is
48	# pinned <1.24 for torch-1.11 ABI compatibility.
49	python -m pip install -q hydra-core omegaconf pandas opencv-python-headless pillow scipy tqdm pyyaml matplotlib "numpy<1.24" || true
50	
51	echo "[gpu] [4/5] pretrained badminton weights"
52	WEIGHTS="$WASB_REPO/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar"
53	if [ -f "$WEIGHTS" ]; then
54	  echo "[gpu]       found $WEIGHTS"
55	else
56	  echo "[gpu]       !! MISSING $WEIGHTS — fetch wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar (Drive/bucket) before extraction."
57	fi
58	
59	echo "[gpu] [5/5] GPU sanity check (in the wasb env)"
60	python - <<'PY'
61	import torch
62	ok = torch.cuda.is_available()
63	print("[gpu]       torch", torch.__version__, "| cuda_available", ok,
64	      "| device", (torch.cuda.get_device_name(0) if ok else "CPU"))
65	assert ok, "CUDA not available in the wasb env — check the GPU driver / base image"
66	PY
67	
68	echo "[gpu] native WASB env ready.  Activate with:  source $CONDA_DIR/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate wasb"
69	echo "[gpu] (the LinuxNativeRunner — backend/pipeline/detectors — drives extraction once it lands; M3.5.)"
70

### 15. user (2026-06-20T15:58:14.853Z)

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	#
3	# Detect a spare local NVMe and mount it at /scratch for fast video decode.
4	#
5	# DO GPU Droplets ship a SECOND raw NVMe (the "scratch" disk), unmounted by default; a
6	# regular CPU box has only the boot disk — then we just create /scratch on it. The hot path
7	# is sync-first (copy video local -> ffmpeg-decode there), so scratch belongs on the fast
8	# local disk. Point the pipeline at it with  export TMPDIR=/scratch  or  --scratch /scratch.
9	#
10	# EPHEMERAL by design (wiped on destroy / may not survive a power-cycle) and deliberately NOT
11	# added to /etc/fstab (a renamed device could block boot). Nothing durable lives here.
12	set -euo pipefail
13	MNT=/scratch
14	mkdir -p "$MNT"
15	
16	root_src=$(findmnt -n -o SOURCE / 2>/dev/null || true)
17	root_disk=$(lsblk -no PKNAME "$root_src" 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)
18	[ -z "$root_disk" ] && root_disk=$(basename "${root_src:-}" | sed -E 's/p?[0-9]+$//')
19	
20	cand=""
21	while read -r name type; do
22	  [ "$type" = "disk" ] || continue
23	  [ "$name" = "$root_disk" ] && continue
24	  bytes=$(lsblk -bdno SIZE "/dev/$name" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
25	  [ "${bytes:-0}" -lt 10000000000 ] && continue            # skip tiny config drives (<10 GB)
26	  if lsblk -no MOUNTPOINT "/dev/$name" | grep -q '/'; then continue; fi   # skip if already mounted
27	  cand="$name"; break
28	done < <(lsblk -dno NAME,TYPE)
29	
30	if [ -n "$cand" ]; then
31	  dev="/dev/$cand"
32	  echo "[scratch] spare disk: $dev"
33	  if ! blkid "$dev" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
34	    echo "[scratch] no filesystem -> mkfs.ext4 $dev"
35	    mkfs.ext4 -F -q "$dev"
36	  fi
37	  mountpoint -q "$MNT" || mount "$dev" "$MNT"
38	  chmod 1777 "$MNT"
39	  echo "[scratch] mounted $dev at $MNT — $(df -h "$MNT" | awk 'NR==2{print $2" total, "$4" free"}')"
40	else
41	  echo "[scratch] no spare NVMe found — using $MNT on the boot disk (fine for a CPU box)"
42	  chmod 1777 "$MNT"
43	fi
44	
45	echo
46	echo ">> Point the pipeline at scratch:  export TMPDIR=$MNT   (or pass --scratch $MNT to backend.worker)"
47

1	# Testing the rally pipeline on DigitalOcean
2	
3	A storage-free, secret-free way to get the pipeline running on a DigitalOcean (or Paperspace)
4	machine — the **compute** half of [`docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/`](../../docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/README.md).
5	The object-store **storage shim is intentionally out of scope here** (owner is finalizing it); this
6	runbook only proves *compute* works on the box.
7	
8	> ⚠ The commands below mirror `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (Ubuntu) so the box matches CI, but the
9	> `bootstrap.sh` script has **not yet been run on a real DO box** — treat it as a tested-by-mirroring
10	> starting point and adjust as you go.
11	
12	## 1. Credentials to create (least-privilege)
13	
14	| # | Credential | Where to create | Scope | Needed |
15	|---|---|---|---|---|
16	| 1 | **SSH keypair** | local `ssh-keygen`; add the **public** key to DO (Settings → Security) / Paperspace | machine login | **now** |
17	| 2 | **DigitalOcean API token** | DO console → API → Tokens → Generate (Personal Access Token) | **read + write** (Droplets/Spaces); scope down if the UI allows | **now** (for `doctl`; optional if you click-create the box) |
18	| 3 | **Paperspace / Gradient API key** | console.paperspace.com → API Keys | account | **for the GPU box** (free M4000 / cheap A4000 live under Paperspace) |
19	| 4 | **GitHub repo read access** on the box | a **read-only deploy key** (repo → Settings → Deploy keys) *or* a **fine-grained PAT** with `Contents: Read` | this repo only | **now** (to `git clone`) |
20	| 5 | **DO Spaces keys** (access key + secret) | DO console → API → **Spaces Keys** | one bucket/prefix | **defer** — only when the storage shim + a real data test land |
21	| 6 | **`GEMINI_API_KEY`** | Google AI Studio (or Vertex) | the Gemini segmenter | **later** — only to test the *Gemini* path; the stub/WASB path needs none. **Rotate the old key that was shared in chat.** |
22	
23	**The "API key that drives the GPU"** in the North-Star sense is **#2 (DO token)** + **#3 (Paperspace key)** —
24	they let you (or `doctl`/a control plane) spin up and drive GPU machines. The per-invocation *serverless*
25	endpoint key (RunPod / Cloud Run) is a later M3 concern.
26	
27	**Secret hygiene:** never bake any of these into the repo or a Docker image. Put them in the box's
28	environment / a `.env` that is git-ignored, or the provider's secret store. Prefer scoping #2 and #4 to the
29	narrowest resource the UI allows.
30	
31	## 2. Bring up a box
32	
33	- **CPU smoke (M0/M1-cpu):** the cheapest **Droplet** (a basic shared-CPU Ubuntu 22.04/24.04) — or, free, the
34	  **Paperspace Gradient M4000** GPU machine (unbilled on the Free plan).
35	- **GPU test (M1 / M3.5 WSL→native parity):** a **Paperspace GPU** (free **M4000**, then **P4000/RTX4000/A4000**
36	  on credit) or a **GPU Droplet**. All are Ubuntu + NVIDIA driver + CUDA.
37	
38	`doctl` (optional) once #2 is set: `doctl auth init` then `doctl compute droplet create …`. Or just create it
39	in the console and copy the IP.
40	
41	## 3. Bootstrap + smoke (on the box)
42	
43	```bash
44	ssh user@<box-ip>
45	git clone <repo>           # via deploy key (#4)
46	cd badminton-highlight-indexer
47	
48	./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh          # CPU box
49	#   or
50	./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh --gpu     # GPU box (installs CUDA torch)
51	
52	. .venv/bin/activate && python -m pytest -q   # storage-free, secret-free smoke (~900 passed)
53	```
54	
55	**Acceptance:**
56	- CPU box → the mocked suite passes (the whole pipeline runs with no GPU/WSL/bucket/key).
57	- GPU box → the bootstrap device check prints `cuda_available True` + the GPU name (M1 acceptance).
58	
59	Once [#247](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/247) (the CPU-mock detector seam)
60	is merged, you can also drive the trajectory segmenter GPU-free end-to-end:
61	`RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=stub python -m pytest tests/test_stub_runner.py -q`.
62	
63	## 4. Next compute steps (not in this runbook)
64	
65	- **M3.5 WSL→native detector parity** — replicate the `TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md` WASB conda env *natively*
66	  on the GPU box (no WSL), add a `LinuxNativeRunner`, and parity-test its trajectory CSV vs the WSL box.
67	- **`train` / `infer` dispatcher + the bucket** — gated on the storage shim (owner-finalizing).
68

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Provision the DECOUPLED_COMPUTE GCP storage + IAM (asia-south1 / Mumbai). Idempotent: safe to
3	# re-run; already-existing resources are left as-is. The GPU VM is NOT created here (it is gated on
4	# the GPUS_ALL_REGIONS quota — see deploy/gcp/README.md §2-3); create it with the §3 command once
5	# the quota is granted. This documents EXACTLY what was run on 2026-06-20 (DO->GCP pivot, ADR-010).
6	#
7	# Prereq: `gcloud auth login`; Service Usage API + a linked billing account enabled in the console
8	# (README §1). Run: bash deploy/gcp/provision.sh
9	set -uo pipefail
10	
11	PROJECT="${RALLY_GCP_PROJECT:-gen-lang-client-0306026826}"
12	REGION="${RALLY_GCP_REGION:-asia-south1}"
13	BUCKET="${RALLY_GCS_BUCKET:-khelsutra-rally-corpus}"
14	BILLING_ACCT="${RALLY_BILLING_ACCT:-01420D-419EE0-53D83C}"
15	SA_NAME="rally-worker"
16	SA_EMAIL="${SA_NAME}@${PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
17	SA_KEY="${RALLY_SA_KEY:-$HOME/.gcp/rally-worker-sa.json}"
18	export CLOUDSDK_CORE_DISABLE_PROMPTS=1
19	
20	echo "== project=$PROJECT region=$REGION bucket=gs://$BUCKET =="
21	
22	echo "== enable APIs =="
23	gcloud services enable compute.googleapis.com cloudbilling.googleapis.com \
24	  billingbudgets.googleapis.com iam.googleapis.com cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com \
25	  --project "$PROJECT"
26	
27	echo "== GCS bucket (asia-south1, uniform bucket-level access) =="
28	gcloud storage buckets create "gs://$BUCKET" --location="$REGION" \
29	  --uniform-bucket-level-access --project "$PROJECT" || echo "  (bucket exists — ok)"
30	
31	echo "== service account + Storage Object Admin + key =="
32	gcloud iam service-accounts create "$SA_NAME" --display-name="Rally worker (storage)" \
33	  --project "$PROJECT" || echo "  (SA exists — ok)"
34	gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding "$PROJECT" \
35	  --member="serviceAccount:$SA_EMAIL" --role="roles/storage.objectAdmin" --condition=None \
36	  --format="value(etag)" >/dev/null
37	if [ ! -f "$SA_KEY" ]; then
38	  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$SA_KEY")"
39	  gcloud iam service-accounts keys create "$SA_KEY" --iam-account="$SA_EMAIL"
40	  echo "  key -> $SA_KEY   (NEVER commit this; export GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=$SA_KEY)"
41	else
42	  echo "  key already at $SA_KEY — leaving it (delete to rotate)"
43	fi
44	
45	echo "== \$100 budget alert (50/90/100%) =="
46	gcloud billing budgets create --billing-account="$BILLING_ACCT" \
47	  --display-name="rally-100usd-guard" --budget-amount=100 \
48	  --filter-projects="projects/$PROJECT" \
49	  --threshold-rule=percent=0.5 --threshold-rule=percent=0.9 --threshold-rule=percent=1.0 \
50	  || echo "  (budget may already exist — check: gcloud billing budgets list --billing-account=$BILLING_ACCT)"
51	
52	echo "== GPU quota (the VM gate) =="
53	echo "  regional $REGION:"
54	gcloud compute regions describe "$REGION" --project "$PROJECT" \
55	  --flatten="quotas[]" --format="table(quotas.metric,quotas.limit)" | grep -E "NVIDIA_(T4|L4)_GPUS " || true
56	echo "  global:"
57	gcloud compute project-info describe --project "$PROJECT" \
58	  --flatten="quotas[]" --format="table(quotas.metric,quotas.limit)" | grep GPUS_ALL_REGIONS || true
59	echo ""
60	echo "If GPUS_ALL_REGIONS == 0, request an increase (README §2), then create the VM (README §3)."
61

### 26. user (2026-06-20T16:15:18.307Z)

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Switch OFF the billable GCP resources between sessions (cost control). The GPU VM is the only
3	# meaningful cost; STOP it (keeps the disk, ~$0 compute) for a fast resume, or DELETE it for $0.
4	# The GCS bucket is NEVER auto-deleted (it holds the corpus) — drop it manually when truly done.
5	#
6	# Usage:
7	#   bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh stop     # stop the VM (default; fastest resume)
8	#   bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh delete   # delete the VM (no disk cost)
9	#   bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh all      # delete VM + SA + budget (keeps the bucket/data)
10	set -uo pipefail
11	
12	PROJECT="${RALLY_GCP_PROJECT:-gen-lang-client-0306026826}"
13	ZONE="${RALLY_GCP_ZONE:-asia-south1-a}"
14	VM="${RALLY_GPU_VM:-rally-gpu}"
15	SA_EMAIL="rally-worker@${PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
16	BILLING_ACCT="${RALLY_BILLING_ACCT:-01420D-419EE0-53D83C}"
17	export CLOUDSDK_CORE_DISABLE_PROMPTS=1
18	ACTION="${1:-stop}"
19	
20	case "$ACTION" in
21	  stop)
22	    echo "== stopping $VM (disk retained; resume with: gcloud compute instances start $VM --zone=$ZONE) =="
23	    gcloud compute instances stop "$VM" --zone="$ZONE" --project "$PROJECT" || echo "  (no running VM named $VM)"
24	    ;;
25	  delete)
26	    echo "== deleting $VM (disk freed; full re-port to resume) =="
27	    gcloud compute instances delete "$VM" --zone="$ZONE" --project "$PROJECT" --quiet || echo "  (no VM named $VM)"
28	    ;;
29	  all)
30	    echo "== deleting VM + SA + budget (bucket/data kept) =="
31	    gcloud compute instances delete "$VM" --zone="$ZONE" --project "$PROJECT" --quiet || echo "  (no VM)"
32	    gcloud iam service-accounts delete "$SA_EMAIL" --project "$PROJECT" --quiet || echo "  (no SA)"
33	    bid=$(gcloud billing budgets list --billing-account="$BILLING_ACCT" \
34	          --filter="displayName=rally-100usd-guard" --format="value(name)" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
35	    [ -n "$bid" ] && gcloud billing budgets delete "$bid" --billing-account="$BILLING_ACCT" --quiet || echo "  (no budget)"
36	    echo "  bucket gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus left intact (delete manually: gcloud storage rm -r gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus)"
37	    ;;
38	  *)
39	    echo "usage: $0 {stop|delete|all}"; exit 2 ;;
40	esac
41	echo "done."
42

196	    """Build the trajectory DetectorRunner for `--trajectory-source detector`, honouring

198	    stub=CPU mock). Returns the runner, or prints an error + returns None.
199	
200	    Reuses the segmenter's `_resolve_runner` seam so the worker and the live CLI build the
201	    detector identically (one source of truth). A non-trajectory segmenter (e.g. yolo) has

226	        return None

231	def cmd_train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
232	    """Gen-0 train-from-bucket: pull labels (+ videos) -> trajectories -> manifest -> shell out
233	    to training.gen0.harness (backend must NOT import training) -> push the artifact bundle.
234	
235	    Two trajectory sources (the train pipeline + harness are identical either way):
236	      * ``synth`` (default) — the CPU "mock GPU": deterministic, label-consistent synthetic
237	        shuttle paths (no GPU/WSL), so the whole pipeline is validated on a CPU box / CI.
238	      * ``detector`` — REAL shuttle trajectories: pull each video and run the resolved
239	        DetectorRunner (native WASB on a GPU box, or stub for a GPU-free wiring test). This
240	        is the M3.5 "first real training" path; only the trajectory source flips.
241	    """
242	    import subprocess
243	
244	    config = load_config(args.config) if args.config else load_config()
245	    _apply_overrides(config, args.set)
246	    storage_cfg = config.storage.model_dump()
247	
248	    scratch = args.scratch or tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="rally-train-")
249	    labels_dir = os.path.join(scratch, "labels")
250	    traj_dir = os.path.join(scratch, "trajectories")
251	    videos_dir = os.path.join(scratch, "videos")
252	    os.makedirs(labels_dir, exist_ok=True)
253	    os.makedirs(traj_dir, exist_ok=True)
254	
255	    corpus = args.corpus_uri.rstrip("/")
256	    label_uris = sorted(u for u in storage.list_uris(f"{corpus}/labels/", config=storage_cfg)
257	                        if u.lower().endswith(".csv"))
258	    if len(label_uris) < 2:
259	        print(f"[worker] need >=2 labeled videos for leave-one-video-out; found {len(label_uris)} "
260	              f"under {corpus}/labels/")
261	        return 2
262	
263	    # Real-detector source: resolve the runner once + index the bucket's videos/ by stem.
264	    runner = None
265	    video_by_stem: dict = {}
266	    if args.trajectory_source == "detector":
267	        os.makedirs(videos_dir, exist_ok=True)
268	        runner = _resolve_train_detector(args, config)
269	        if runner is None:
270	            return 2
271	        for vuri in storage.list_uris(f"{corpus}/videos/", config=storage_cfg):
272	            vname = storage.parse_uri(vuri).name
273	            if not vname.lower().endswith(_VIDEO_EXTS):
274	                continue  # skip sidecars (.srt/.json/thumbnails) so they can't shadow the video
275	            video_by_stem[os.path.splitext(vname)[0]] = vuri
276	
277	    print(f"[worker] trajectory source: {args.trajectory_source}")
278	    specs: List[dict] = []
279	    for uri in label_uris:
280	        fname = storage.parse_uri(uri).name                    # e.g. GX020094_proxy.rallies.csv
281	        name = fname.replace(".rallies.csv", "").replace(".csv", "")
282	        local_label = storage.fetch(uri, os.path.join(labels_dir, fname), config=storage_cfg)
283	        if args.trajectory_source == "detector":
284	            vuri = video_by_stem.get(name)
285	            if not vuri:
286	                print(f"[worker] no video for {name!r} under {corpus}/videos/ — skipping.")
287	                continue
288	            local_video = storage.fetch(vuri, os.path.join(videos_dir, storage.parse_uri(vuri).name),
289	                                        config=storage_cfg)
290	            video_id = compute_video_id(local_video)
291	            # Real fps/width drive the trajectory frame->time mapping; PROBE (don't guess) — a
292	            # wrong fps silently mis-aligns every label, so skip the video if ffprobe can't read it.
293	            meta = _probe_video_fps_width(local_video)
294	            if meta is None:
295	                print(f"[worker] could not probe fps/width for {name!r} (ffprobe) — skipping (won't guess).")
296	                continue
297	            fps, frame_width = meta
298	            traj = runner.run_predict(local_video, traj_dir, video_id=video_id)
299	            if not traj:
300	                print(f"[worker] detector produced no trajectory for {name!r} — skipping.")
301	                continue
302	            specs.append({"name": name, "trajectory": traj, "labels": local_label,
303	                          "fps": fps, "frame_width": frame_width})
304	        else:
305	            from backend.pipeline.detectors.stub_runner import synth_trajectory_from_labels
306	            traj = os.path.join(traj_dir, f"{name}_traj.csv")
307	            synth_trajectory_from_labels(local_label, traj, fps=args.fps, frame_width=args.frame_width)
308	            specs.append({"name": name, "trajectory": traj, "labels": local_label,
309	                          "fps": args.fps, "frame_width": args.frame_width})
310	
311	    if len(specs) < 2:
312	        print(f"[worker] need >=2 videos with trajectories for leave-one-video-out; got {len(specs)}.")
313	        return 2
314	    manifest_path = os.path.join(scratch, "manifest.json")
315	    with open(manifest_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
316	        json.dump(specs, f, indent=2)
317	    src_label = "real detector" if args.trajectory_source == "detector" else "CPU-mock stub"
318	    print(f"[worker] built manifest: {len(specs)} videos ({src_label} trajectories)")
319	
320	    out_dir = os.path.join(scratch, "artifacts")
321	    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "training.gen0.harness",
322	           "--manifest", manifest_path, "--out", out_dir, "--version", args.version]
323	    if args.floor:
324	        floor_local = (storage.fetch(args.floor, os.path.join(scratch, "floor.json"), config=storage_cfg)
325	                       if "://" in args.floor else args.floor)
326	        cmd += ["--floor", floor_local]
327	    print("[worker] training:", " ".join(cmd))
328	    rc = subprocess.run(cmd).returncode
329	    if rc != 0:
330	        print(f"[worker] gen0 harness failed (rc={rc})")
331	        return rc
332	
333	    # PUSH the versioned artifact bundle (weights.json + manifest.json) to the bucket.
334	    bundle = os.path.join(out_dir, args.version)
335	    out_prefix = args.out_uri.rstrip("/")
336	    pushed = []
337	    for fn in sorted(os.listdir(bundle)):
338	        uri = f"{out_prefix}/weights/{args.version}/{fn}"
339	        storage.put(os.path.join(bundle, fn), uri, config=storage_cfg)
340	        pushed.append(uri)
341	    print(f"[worker] train done: pushed {len(pushed)} artifact(s):")
342	    for u in pushed:
343	        print("   ", u)
344	    return 0
345	
346	
347	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
348	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m backend.worker",
349	                                description="Storage-aware worker: pull → run pipeline → push.")
350	    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
351	
352	    pi = sub.add_parser("infer", help="run inference on one video URI and push outputs")
353	    pi.add_argument("--video-uri", required=True, help="local path / local:// / s3:// / gcs://")
354	    pi.add_argument("--out-uri", required=True, help="output prefix (outputs/<video_id>/… is appended)")
355	    pi.add_argument("--segmenter", default=None, help="default: indexing.default_segmenter")
356	    pi.add_argument("--config", default=None, help="config.json path (default: ./config.json)")
357	    pi.add_argument("--scratch", default=None, help="local scratch dir (default: a temp dir)")
358	    pi.add_argument("--max-frames", type=int, default=None)
359	    pi.add_argument("--set", action="append", default=[], metavar="k=v",
360	                    help="config override, e.g. --set indexing.skip_ai_handoff=true")
361	    pi.set_defaults(func=cmd_infer)
362	
363	    pt = sub.add_parser("train", help="Gen-0 train-from-bucket (labels [+videos] -> trajectories -> harness)")
364	    pt.add_argument("--corpus-uri", required=True, help="prefix containing labels/ (+ videos/ for "
365	                    "--trajectory-source detector), e.g. s3://bucket or gcs://bucket")
366	    pt.add_argument("--out-uri", required=True, help="output prefix (weights/<version>/ is appended)")
367	    pt.add_argument("--version", required=True, help="semver for the artifact bundle")
368	    pt.add_argument("--trajectory-source", choices=["synth", "detector"], default="synth",
369	                    help="synth = CPU-mock label-consistent trajectories (default, no GPU); "

6	Linux GPU box (GCP / a SaaS worker): it runs the **same** verified ``wasb_infer.py`` core
7	that the WSL adapter runs, so the trajectory CSV is identical (to a sub-pixel float tolerance;
8	see the acceptance gate below) — but WITHOUT the WSL transport:
9	
10	  * NO ``WslCommandMixin`` (no ``wsl -d <distro> bash -c``), NO ``conda activate`` (the
11	    ``wasb`` env's python is invoked **by path**), NO ``/mnt/c`` path translation
12	    (``to_wsl_mnt_path``). Paths are native; the video is already local on the box.
13	  * ``device`` is ``cuda`` (default — the WSL parity path), ``mps`` or ``cpu``; threaded
14	    into ``wasb_infer.py --device`` (and so into the Hydra ``runner.device`` override).
15	  * weights / repo / python / scratch come from the **environment** first
16	    (``WASB_WEIGHTS`` / ``WASB_REPO_DIR`` / ``WASB_PYTHON`` / ``WASB_SCRATCH``), then
17	    ``indexing.wasb.*`` config, then a default — so a box needs no config edits.
18	
19	It emits the identical ``Frame,Visibility,X,Y`` CSV (same ``{stem}__{vid12}_wasb.csv``
20	name as the WSL runner) and reuses the model-agnostic ``parse_trajectory_csv`` /
21	``trajectory_to_action_windows`` from ``tracknet_runner``, so the trajectory hybrids and
22	the windowing brain are unchanged. Selected via the existing ``detector_impl`` seam
23	(``trajectory_hybrid._resolve_runner`` → ``_build_native_runner``); ``detector_impl="native"``.
24	
25	**Acceptance gate (parity, NOT strict byte-equality):** the SAME clip through the WSL runner
26	(Windows) and this runner (Linux, ``device=cuda``) yields the same trajectory CSV + the same
27	candidate windows **within a small float tolerance (~1e-3 px)**. cuDNN is non-deterministic
28	ACROSS GPUs, so a sub-pixel diff is expected on different hardware — but it is byte-exact
29	run-to-run on a FIXED GPU. ✅ Validated 2026-06-20 on an RTX 2070 (via WSL): native streaming
30	vs the cached WSL-disk CSV matched **1194/1195** frames (the lone diff a ~5e-5 px edge
31	artifact), and two native runs were byte-identical (deterministic). This is a hardware check
32	(a real GPU + the ``wasb`` env), so the offline, subprocess-mocked suite asserts the contract,
33	not the numbers.
34	"""
35	import os
36	import shutil
37	import logging
38	import subprocess
39	from dataclasses import dataclass
40	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
41	
42	from backend.pipeline.detectors.base import DetectorRunner
43	# Reuse the model-agnostic helpers — trajectory shape + windowing are identical to WSL/TrackNet.
44	from backend.pipeline.detectors.tracknet_runner import TrackNetRunner

46	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
47	
48	# Path to the inference core we copy into the WASB repo's src/ (so it can import WASB's
49	# detectors/trackers/dataloaders + find configs/), exactly as the WSL adapter does.
50	_INFER_PY = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "wasb_infer.py")
51	
52	
53	@dataclass
54	class NativeWasbConfig:
55	    """Resolved settings for a Linux-native WASB run.
56	
57	    Built via :meth:`from_indexing_cfg` with **environment overrides** taking precedence
58	    over ``indexing.wasb.*`` config (the box sets env; no config edits needed). ``device``
59	    defaults to ``cuda`` (the WSL parity path); ``stream_video`` defaults to True (the perf win).
60	    """
61	    repo_dir: str = "~/models/WASB-SBDT"   # native clone of nttcom/WASB-SBDT
62	    python_bin: str = "python"             # the `wasb` conda env python (invoked by path, no activate)
63	    weights_path: str = ""                 # REQUIRED (env WASB_WEIGHTS or indexing.wasb.weights_path)
64	    sport: str = "badminton"
65	    device: str = "cuda"                   # cuda | mps | cpu
66	    scratch_dir: str = ""                  # frame-extraction scratch (env); "" = next to the output dir
67	    timeout_sec: int = 1800               # 30 min; a hung GPU call is killed (0 = no timeout)
68	    keep_frames: bool = False              # retain the decoded frame cache after success (debug only)
69	    # NATIVE DEFAULT = STREAMING: skip the cv2 PNG extraction that dominates a long clip's
70	    # wall-clock (measured ~50s on a 20s clip) — bit-identical to disk (verified on a real GPU
71	    # 2026-06-20). The GPU box reads from a local NVMe so streaming is the right default here.
72	    stream_video: bool = True
73	
74	    @classmethod
75	    def from_indexing_cfg(cls, idx_cfg: Dict[str, Any]) -> "NativeWasbConfig":
76	        w = (idx_cfg or {}).get("wasb", {}) or {}
77	        env = os.environ.get
78	        # stream_video is tri-state in config: None/absent => native default (stream); an
79	        # explicit True/False still wins (e.g. False for a container cv2 can't seek).
80	        sv = w.get("stream_video")
81	        return cls(
82	            repo_dir=env("WASB_REPO_DIR") or w.get("repo_dir", "~/models/WASB-SBDT"),
83	            python_bin=env("WASB_PYTHON") or w.get("python_bin", "python"),
84	            weights_path=env("WASB_WEIGHTS") or w.get("weights_path", "") or "",
85	            sport=w.get("sport", "badminton"),
86	            device=env("WASB_DEVICE") or w.get("device", "cuda"),
87	            scratch_dir=env("WASB_SCRATCH") or env("RALLY_SCRATCH") or env("TMPDIR") or "",
88	            timeout_sec=int(w.get("timeout_sec", 1800)),
89	            keep_frames=bool(w.get("keep_frames", False)),
90	            stream_video=(True if sv is None else bool(sv)),
91	        )
92	
93	
94	class NativeWasbRunner(DetectorRunner):
95	    """WASB runner that executes natively on a Linux GPU box — no WSL. See module docstring."""
96	
97	    def __init__(self, cfg: NativeWasbConfig):
98	        self.cfg = cfg
99	
100	    # --- low-level native exec (the single place tests patch) --------------------------
101	    def _run(self, argv: List[str], cwd: Optional[str] = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
102	        logger.debug("native exec: %s (cwd=%s)", " ".join(argv), cwd)
103	        return subprocess.run(argv, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True,
104	                              timeout=(self.cfg.timeout_sec or None))
105	
106	    def _repo_src(self) -> str:
107	        return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser(self.cfg.repo_dir), "src")
108	
109	    def _copy_infer_script(self) -> bool:
110	        """Copy wasb_infer.py into the WASB repo src/ so it imports WASB modules + finds configs/."""
111	        repo_src = self._repo_src()
112	        try:
113	            os.makedirs(repo_src, exist_ok=True)
114	            shutil.copy(_INFER_PY, os.path.join(repo_src, "wasb_infer.py"))
115	            return True
116	        except OSError as e:
117	            logger.error("Failed to copy wasb_infer.py into %s: %s", repo_src, e)
118	            return False
119	
120	    def _rm_rf(self, path: Optional[str]) -> None:
121	        """Best-effort recursive delete of a native path (post-success cleanup; never raises)."""
122	        if path:
123	            shutil.rmtree(path, ignore_errors=True)
124	
125	    def healthcheck(self) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
126	        """Verify weights + repo present and the `wasb` python imports torch (and sees a GPU on cuda)."""
127	        if not self.cfg.weights_path:
128	            return False, ("WASB weights path is empty — set WASB_WEIGHTS (or indexing.wasb.weights_path).")
129	        weights = os.path.expanduser(self.cfg.weights_path)
130	        if not os.path.exists(weights):
131	            return False, f"WASB weights not found at {weights}"
132	        repo_src = self._repo_src()
133	        if not os.path.isdir(repo_src):
134	            return False, (f"WASB-SBDT repo src/ not found at {repo_src} — clone nttcom/WASB-SBDT "
135	                           f"(set WASB_REPO_DIR / indexing.wasb.repo_dir).")
136	        code = "import torch; print('torch', torch.__version__, 'cuda', torch.cuda.is_available())"
137	        try:
138	            res = self._run([self.cfg.python_bin, "-c", code])
139	        except FileNotFoundError:
140	            return False, f"python binary not found: {self.cfg.python_bin!r} (set WASB_PYTHON)."
141	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
142	            return False, "native torch healthcheck timed out."
143	        out = (res.stdout or "").strip()
144	        if res.returncode != 0:
145	            return False, f"`{self.cfg.python_bin}` torch import failed: {(res.stderr or out).strip()}"
146	        if self.cfg.device == "cuda" and "cuda True" not in out:
147	            return False, f"device=cuda but torch.cuda.is_available() is False ({out})"
148	        return True, out
149	
150	    def run_predict(self, video_win_path: str, output_win_dir: str,
151	                    video_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:
152	        """Run WASB natively on a video. Returns the path to the trajectory CSV, or None.
153	
154	        Output artifacts are namespaced by ``video_id`` (the file MD5) exactly as the WSL
155	        runner does, and the CSV is named ``{stem}__{vid12}_wasb.csv`` — byte-for-byte the
156	        same downstream contract, so the parity comparison is apples-to-apples.
157	        """
158	        output_dir = os.path.abspath(output_win_dir)
159	        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
160	        # Normalize for a cross-platform basename (tests pass Windows paths on Linux).
161	        norm = str(video_win_path).replace("\\", "/")
162	        stem, _ext = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(norm))
163	        # Content-derived key: same filename + different bytes -> different key (no cache reuse).
164	        key = f"{stem}__{video_id[:12]}" if video_id else (stem or "wasb")
165	        out_csv = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{key}_wasb.csv")
166	
167	        if not self._copy_infer_script():
168	            logger.error("Could not stage wasb_infer.py into the WASB repo src/.")
169	            return None
170	
171	        weights = os.path.expanduser(self.cfg.weights_path)
172	        argv = [self.cfg.python_bin, "wasb_infer.py",
173	                "--video", os.path.abspath(str(video_win_path))]
174	        frames_dir: Optional[str] = None
175	        if self.cfg.stream_video:
176	            # Fast path: decode in-process, no PNG extraction. Output is bit-identical to disk.
177	            argv.append("--stream-video")
178	        else:
179	            scratch = self.cfg.scratch_dir or output_dir
180	            frames_dir = os.path.join(scratch, f"{key}_frames")
181	            argv += ["--frames_out_dir", frames_dir]
182	        argv += ["--weights", weights,
183	                 "--sport", self.cfg.sport,
184	                 "--device", self.cfg.device,
185	                 "--out", out_csv]
186	
187	        logger.info("Running native WASB inference (device=%s) on %s ...",
188	                    self.cfg.device, os.path.basename(norm))
189	        try:
190	            res = self._run(argv, cwd=self._repo_src())
191	        except FileNotFoundError:
192	            logger.error("python binary not found: %r (set WASB_PYTHON).", self.cfg.python_bin)
193	            return None
194	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
195	            logger.error("WASB native inference timed out after %ss.", self.cfg.timeout_sec)
196	            return None
197	        if res.returncode != 0:
198	            logger.error("WASB native inference failed:\n%s", (res.stderr or res.stdout or "")[-2000:])
199	            return None
200	        if not os.path.exists(out_csv):
201	            logger.error("WASB native finished but expected CSV not found at %s", out_csv)
202	            return None
203	        logger.info("WASB trajectory CSV: %s", out_csv)
204	
205	        # Success → the CSV is the durable output; the decoded frame cache is a pure intermediate.
206	        # On any earlier failure we return above WITHOUT cleaning, so the cache survives a resume.
207	        # (Streaming never materializes frames, so there is nothing to drop on that path.)
208	        if not self.cfg.keep_frames and frames_dir:
209	            logger.info("Cache hygiene: removing frame cache for %s "
210	                        "(set indexing.wasb.keep_frames=true to retain).", key)
211	            self._rm_rf(frames_dir)
212	        return out_csv
213	
214	    # Trajectory parsing + windowing are inherited from tracknet_runner (model-agnostic).
215	    parse_trajectory_csv = staticmethod(TrackNetRunner.parse_trajectory_csv)
216	    trajectory_to_action_windows = staticmethod(TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows)

- **`stream_video=false`** (disk extraction — finding #6, streaming starved the GPU on long videos)
- **`timeout_sec=10800`** (the default 30 min is too tight for long 59.94 fps clips — prior handoff finding)

Weights land at `gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/weights/0.1.0-l4/` (matching the documented target). The 2 staged pairs (GX030094_proxy + mahadevpura-1) are exactly what the leave-one-video-out train needs.

### Provisioning the L4 (the exact command, per confirm-first)

```
gcloud compute instances create rally-gpu \
  --project=gen-lang-client-0306026826 --zone=asia-south1-c \
  --machine-type=g2-standard-4 \              # L4 built-in, ~$0.70–0.85/hr
  --maintenance-policy=TERMINATE --provisioning-model=STANDARD \
  --image-family=common-cu129-ubuntu-2204-nvidia-580 \
  --image-project=deeplearning-platform-release \
  --boot-disk-size=120GB --boot-disk-type=pd-balanced \
  --local-ssd=interface=NVME \                # 375GB fast /scratch for disk extraction
  --service-account=rally-worker@gen-lang-client-0306026826.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
  --scopes=cloud-platform
```

Run ~1–2 hr ⇒ **~$1–2**; `$100` budget guard live; **VM deleted at the end**. Creating now.

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One-shot box setup for the M2 finalize run (GCP L4). Idempotent-ish; run nohup'd.
set -uo pipefail
cd ~/rally || { echo "NO ~/rally"; exit 1; }
echo "===== [setup] $(date) start ====="
nvidia-smi -L || echo "(nvidia-smi not ready yet)"

echo "===== [setup] mount scratch ====="
sudo bash deploy/digitalocean/mount_scratch.sh || { echo "SCRATCH_FAIL"; exit 1; }

echo "===== [setup] bootstrap --gpu (venv + torch cu124) ====="
./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh --gpu || { echo "BOOTSTRAP_FAIL"; exit 1; }

echo "===== [setup] pip storage-gcs ====="
. .venv/bin/activate && pip install -e .[storage-gcs] || { echo "GCS_EXTRA_FAIL"; exit 1; }

echo "===== [setup] gpu_setup (native wasb conda env) ====="
bash deploy/digitalocean/gpu_setup.sh || { echo "GPU_SETUP_FAIL"; exit 1; }

echo "===== [setup] stage WASB weights from bucket ====="
mkdir -p ~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights
gcloud storage cp gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/models/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar \
  ~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/ || { echo "WEIGHTS_STAGE_FAIL"; exit 1; }

echo "===== [setup] write m2_env.sh + config.json ====="
WASB_PY="$HOME/miniconda3/envs/wasb/bin/python"
[ -x "$WASB_PY" ] || { echo "NO_WASB_PYTHON at $WASB_PY"; exit 1; }
cat > ~/rally/m2_env.sh <<EOF
export RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=native
export WASB_WEIGHTS=\$HOME/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar
export WASB_REPO_DIR=\$HOME/models/WASB-SBDT
export WASB_PYTHON=$WASB_PY
export WASB_SCRATCH=/scratch
export TMPDIR=/scratch
EOF
cat > ~/rally/config.json <<'EOF'
{ "sport": "badminton",
  "indexing": { "default_segmenter": "wasb_hybrid", "detector_impl": "native",
                "wasb": { "stream_video": false, "timeout_sec": 10800 } },
  "storage": { "backend": "local", "gcs": { "project": "gen-lang-client-0306026826" } } }
EOF
echo "WASB_PYTHON=$WASB_PY"
ls -la ~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/
echo "===== [setup] $(date) DONE ====="
echo "ALL_SETUP_DONE"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# M2 finalize: real-detector leave-one-video-out train -> push weights/0.1.0-l4/. Run nohup'd.
set -uo pipefail
cd ~/rally
. .venv/bin/activate
set -a; . ~/rally/m2_env.sh; set +a
echo "===== [m2] $(date) train start ====="
echo "config:"; cat config.json
python -m backend.worker train \
  --corpus-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus \
  --out-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus \
  --version 0.1.0-l4 \
  --trajectory-source detector \
  --segmenter wasb_hybrid \
  --config config.json \
  --scratch /scratch
rc=$?
echo "===== [m2] $(date) train rc=$rc ====="
echo "M2_TRAIN_DONE rc=$rc"

### 79. assistant (2026-06-20T16:54:23.892Z)

---
name: gcp-ops-agent-enable
description: GCP Ops Agent observability needs 3 things on EVERY GPU VM (APIs + SA roles + agent install) — bake into provisioning; owner has now missed it twice.
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

Owner directive (2026-06-20, the **2nd** time a rally GPU VM had empty Observability graphs): **always enable the GCP Ops Agent** when provisioning a rally GPU VM, and make it AUTOMATIC so it can't be forgotten.

**Why:** the Compute Engine → VM → **Observability → GPU** graphs (and Logs) stay blank unless ALL three pieces below are in place. Each new VM silently starts WITHOUT the agent, so GPU utilization/logs never appear and the owner can't watch the run. Happened on the deleted L4 (#261, "fixed" per the report) and AGAIN on the asia-southeast1 L4 — because I skipped the startup-script on the new box. Treat "spin up a GPU VM" as implying "the Ops Agent is on it."

**How to apply** — three required pieces (deploy/gcp/README §3b):
1. **Project APIs ON:** `gcloud services enable logging.googleapis.com monitoring.googleapis.com` (project-level, persists). This is the real empty-graphs root cause: the agent runs but its exports get `PermissionDenied/SERVICE_DISABLED` until these are on.
2. **Worker SA roles:** `roles/monitoring.metricWriter` + `roles/logging.logWriter` on `rally-worker` (it otherwise only has storage.objectAdmin).
3. **The agent ON THE VM:** `curl -sSO …/add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh && sudo bash add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh --also-install`. **BAKE this into the VM `--metadata=startup-script`** so every box auto-installs it (the console OS-policy install is blocked on DLVM images — that console error is a red herring). If the agent was installed before the APIs were enabled: `sudo systemctl restart google-cloud-ops-agent`.

**Verify:** `sudo systemctl status google-cloud-ops-agent` (active); metrics appear ~2-3 min later under the VM's **Observability** tab, or Metrics Explorer metric `agent.googleapis.com/gpu/utilization` (resource: gce_instance). Codify in `deploy/gcp` so it's not manual: `provision.sh` enables the APIs + grants the SA roles, and the documented `instances create` command carries the Ops-Agent startup-script. [[monetization-plan]]

### 87. assistant (2026-06-20T16:57:18.182Z)

Both long jobs are now running in the background (M2 poller + the workspace-mapping workflow) and will re-invoke me on completion. While they run, here's your TPU question.

## TPU vs GPU — recommendation: **stay GPU-native; don't invest in TPU now**

Grounded in *this* workload and the pay-per-video orchestration model you described:

**1. The workload isn't TPU-shaped.** WASB is a tiny (5.8 MB) PyTorch CNN doing per-frame heatmap inference; the future owned model (Qwen3-VL) is a VLM. TPUs win on large, dense, *batched* matmul at sustained training scale. Streaming per-frame CNN inference with small/dynamic batches is a poor TPU fit. Revisit only if a large owned transformer at sustained batch scale becomes the dominant cost — Gen-2+, owner-gated — and even then, benchmark first.

**2. Your real bottleneck is video decode, not GPU math.** This very run is decode-bound — cv2 PNG extraction at ~12 fps with the **GPU sitting idle during extraction**. TPUs do *nothing* for video decode. The lever that actually helps is **NVDEC** (hardware decode on L4/T4) + parallel decode — i.e., lean *harder* into the GPU, not away from it. That's the single biggest wall-clock win available.

**3. Portability cost is real and cuts against your strategy.** TPU needs XLA (torch_xla/JAX). Porting WASB (torch 1.11) + the pipeline to XLA is significant, fragile NRE, and adds a second runtime. TPU is GCP-only — it would **break the local ↔ VM ↔ Cloud Run parity you're explicitly building** (the decoupled storage/compute seams). One portable PyTorch+CUDA container that runs identically everywhere is worth more than peak FLOPs here.

**4. Economics favor GPU for per-video billing.** The clean fit is **Cloud Run + GPU (L4), scale-to-zero**: pay per request, zero idle-GPU cost, co-located with the GCS bucket, and the L4 has the NVDEC/NVENC codec hardware you need. TPU pricing/provisioning (v5e pods, queued/committed resources) is built for sustained training, not bursty per-video inference — and TPU regional availability is *narrower* than GPU (we just hit GPU stockouts in Mumbai; TPU would be worse).

**Where to invest instead (all GPU-native):** GPU-accelerated decode (NVDEC) for the extraction bottleneck → Cloud Run+GPU scale-to-zero for serving → keep the decoupled seams so the same image runs local/VM/Cloud Run.

**Bottom line:** GPU-native (L4/T4 → Cloud Run+GPU) is right for the foreseeable roadmap. I can record this as a short ADR (e.g. ADR-012: "GPU-native compute; TPU deferred") + a memory note if you want it locked so it isn't relitigated — but I'll hold off until you confirm the direction rather than bank an unratified decision.

---

**In flight now** (I'll report when each fires):
- **M2 train** — extracting frames on the L4 (Singapore); poller will catch `weights/0.1.0-l4/`.
- **Workspace mapping workflow** — fanning out to find every per-video write site, then drafting the single-folder-per-video convention + code-change plan. I'll turn that into a tracked design doc (`docs/PER_VIDEO_WORKSPACE.md`) and start implementation on a branch off `origin/master`.
- **Ops Agent** — fixed on this VM; codifying into `deploy/gcp` so future boxes auto-enable it.

541	  any gcloud provisioning works (gcloud's own `services enable` needs it — chicken-and-egg).
542	- **Cost guardrails:** a `$100` budget alert (`rally-100usd-guard`, 50/90/100%), cheapest adequate
543	  GPU (**T4**), and STOP/DELETE the VM when idle (`deploy/gcp/teardown.sh`). The bucket is never
544	  auto-deleted.
545	- `SETUP_NEW_MACHINE.md` §2's "DO GPU droplet in `sgp1`" is **superseded** by the GCP path
546	  (`deploy/gcp/README.md`). **WASB-C3** (weight provenance) remains the open legal item before
547	  sharing any trajectories/model externally.
548	- The M3.5 **native WASB runner** that makes the GPU box useful landed in **PR #256**
549	  (`detector_impl="native"`); its byte-parity acceptance gate is verified on the GPU box, not in CI.
550	
551	## ADR-011: DECOUPLED_COMPUTE scope = decoupling core (M0–M2 + M3.5); defer serving/billing; DO=training, GCP=serving
552	
553	**Date:** 2026-06-20 · **Status:** RATIFIED (owner, in-session). **Refines ADR-010.**
554	
555	### Decision
556	
557	1. **DECOUPLED_COMPUTE's definition-of-done = the decoupling CORE: M0 (CPU e2e) + M1 (single GPU
558	   run) + M2 (train-from-bucket) + M3.5 (Linux-native detector parity)** — i.e. "the rally pipeline
559	   runs off the local box on a rented GPU, bucket-driven train + infer, with real detectors." It is
560	   **archivable after ONE real GPU run** proves M1/M2/M3.5 on cloud hardware.
561	2. **DEFER M3 (inference serving endpoint + per-video billing) and M4 (scale-to-zero + cost-guard)**
562	   out of DECOUPLED_COMPUTE → into the **monetization / appliance (khelsutra) track.** They are
563	   productization (a cost ledger on the `jobs` table + a non-owner-upload DPA/consent gate), not
564	   compute-portability.
565	3. **Stack split by purpose** (refines ADR-010, which had moved *everything* to GCP):
566	   - **DigitalOcean = TRAINING / the decoupling core.** Use the **$200 DO credits**; DO GPU droplets
567	     are North-America-only (`tor1`/`nyc2`) + co-located DO Spaces (`nyc3`) — NA co-location is fine
568	     for training/dev (no per-customer latency requirement). This is where M1/M2/M3.5 get proven.
569	   - **GCP = the SERVING path to real customers.** Cloud Run GPU (scale-to-zero) gives native
570	     per-invocation GPU-seconds = the exact per-customer/per-video bill (M3/M4, monetization track).
571	     The GCS bucket (`gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus`) + SA (`rally-worker`) + `$100` budget provisioned
572	     under ADR-010 are **RETAINED** for this serving path (do NOT tear them down).
573	
574	### Rationale
575	
576	- The North Star question ("can we lift compute onto a rented GPU doing bucket train+infer?") is
577	  answered by M0–M2 + M3.5; M3/M4 answer a *different* question ("bill a non-owner customer per
578	  video"), which is serverless-shaped and the docs already route to Cloud Run/RunPod, not DO.
579	- Splitting by purpose uses each provider's strength: DO's real credits + 1 TiB-egress Spaces for the
580	  heavy training pulls; GCP's scale-to-zero per-invocation metering for customer billing. (DO has no
581	  serverless GPU → coarse wall-clock billing only; unsuitable for per-customer serving.)
582	
583	### Consequences
584	
585	- **Archive `docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/` only AFTER the DO GPU run is green** (M1/M2/M3.5) — not
586	  aspirationally. Until then it stays a live project doc with this narrowed scope.
587	- M3.5 acceptance is **parity within ~1e-3 px** (not strict byte-equality), validated on an RTX 2070
588	  (native==WSL 1194/1195; deterministic) — see PR #259. **Spot does NOT bypass `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS`**
589	  (tested) — both on-demand + Spot need the quota; a `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS→1` increase was filed via the
590	  Cloud Quotas API (pending). GCP GPU quota matters for the GCP *serving* spike, not the DO training run.
591	- Small carried-forward code item: de-hardcode `"output"` in `trajectory_hybrid.py:237,313` (bucket
592	  cleanliness). **WASB-C3** (weight provenance) follows the model into the monetization track (gates
593	  *sharing* a trained model, not internal decoupling).
594	- The DO training run needs: a DO API token (owner), the rotated Gemini key, WASB weights, corpus
595	  staged to Spaces. The old DO CPU droplet `579001481` is drained + destroyed; the exposed Spaces key
596	  rotated (ADR-010).
597

CACHE HYGIENE: (1) On run_predict success, staged video + frame cache are deleted unless keep_frames=true (WSL: _wsl_rm_rf at wasb_runner.py:194; native: _rm_rf at native_wasb_runner.py:211). CACHE KEYS (wasb_infer.py:137-154): manifest_compatible checks frames_dir (abspath), weights path, sport, frames_in, frames_total, and device.
