### 323. assistant (2026-06-21T02:44:34.808Z)

# Compute-Decoupled Rally Serving — one pure core, profile-selected compute + storage

> **Status:** `DRAFT` — `owner-gated` (design sign-off before code) · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-06-21 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-21
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/past_projects/` when DONE)
> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; pointer in memory `current-state` + `docs/NEXT_STEPS.md` once work starts.
> **Relation to existing docs:** the productionization successor to the archived `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE` (M3/M4); realizes the `ComputeBackend`/`StorageBackend` registries from [PLATFORM_ARCHITECTURE.md](../PLATFORM_ARCHITECTURE.md) §3; the **truly-local** profile is the L0 tier of [VIDEO_LOCALITY_MODEL.md](../VIDEO_LOCALITY_MODEL.md) and the `LocalDesktop` backend of [DESKTOP_APP_PLAN.md](../DESKTOP_APP_PLAN.md).
> **Naming:** this was the "Cloud Run + GPU serving subproject" (ADR-012). Renamed **compute-decoupled serving** because **truly-local is a co-equal, first-class profile**, not an afterthought.
> **Honesty note:** claims tagged `[verified]` (checked against code by the design workflow `wf_56274ff0`), `[design]` (proposed), `[researched]`.

---

## 0. TL;DR
The rally engine is a **pure function of `(input bytes + config) → outputs`** in three deterministic stages — **DETECT → WINDOW → STITCH** — that **no deployment profile may branch on**. A **`ComputeBackend` (deployment profile)** decides *where* the core runs (a local process vs a Cloud Run GPU job); a **`StorageBackend`** decides *where bytes come from* (local FS / USB / mounted Google Drive / bucket). **One startup config** picks both. We ship **truly-local** (local GPU + in-process stitch, zero cloud) and **cloud-serving** (upload `<2GB` / gdrive / bucket → a Cloud Run GPU job runs detect **and** stitch, so stitch's compute is metered), plus **cpu-mock** for CI. The single most important caveat: detection is **byte-identical only on the same GPU** (cross-GPU is sub-pixel-different, per the 2026-06-20 deploy report) — so the **parity guarantee is enforced at the WINDOW + STITCH layer**, not detector-CSV bytes.

## 1. Problem & goal
- **Why now:** DECOUPLED_COMPUTE proved compute *portability* (M0–M2 + M3.5 on a GCP L4) and is archived; its deferred **M3 (serving endpoint + per-video billing) + M4 (scale-to-zero + cost guard)** become this project. The owner needs **both** a hosted service *and* a truly-local mode, with the **core (detect+stitch) unaffected** by which one runs.
- **The two user-facing outcomes** (owner, 2026-06-21):
  - **(a) Hosted service** — operate on small **local uploads (`<2GB`)** *and* load from **gdrive / a bucket**, spinning up **Cloud Run for both rally segmentation AND stitching** (stitching is compute-intensive → run it where its cost is accounted for).
  - **(b) Truly-local** — on a local machine with videos on **local drives/USB + a local GPU**, run inference **and** stitching **completely locally**.
- **"Good" looks like:** the same `video → rallies → reel` core gives identical windows + stitched ranges whether it runs on a laptop or Cloud Run; switching is a **config/startup choice**, never a code branch in the core.

## 2. Decisions locked

| # | Decision | Source | Implication |
|---|----------|--------|-------------|
| D1 | The core is a **pure 3-stage function** (DETECT/WINDOW/STITCH); **no profile branch inside it**. | design `[verified]` | All profile difference lives in config + the two registries. |
| D2 | **Two registries:** `ComputeBackend` (where) selected by `deployment.profile` + `compute_target`; `StorageBackend` (what-bytes) by `input_backend`/`storage.backend`. | ADR-014 | Realizes PLATFORM_ARCHITECTURE's `local\|hosted\|byo_worker`. |
| D3 | **Profiles shipped:** `truly-local`, `cloud-serving`, `cpu-mock` (CI); `byo_worker` reserved/deferred. | ADR-014 | Enum/seam reserved so BYO isn't precluded. |
| D4 | In **cloud-serving, STITCH runs on Cloud Run** (co-located with detect) so CPU/wall + egress are metered into the per-job cost ledger. | owner (a) | Stitch-on-laptop would hide real cost. |
| D5 | **Parity enforced at WINDOW+STITCH** (byte-exact for a fixed trajectory); **detect** is byte-exact only same-GPU. | deploy report 2026-06-20 | Cross-GPU asserted at the rally/window level, never CSV bytes. |
| D6 | **Default-OFF, smallest-safe-first, one PR per milestone**; any core-caller change (e.g. configurable output base) ships behind a flag + a Launch Report. | [[launch-report-convention]] | Zero-regression discipline. |

## 3. Foundation — evolution / ADR lineage  ⟵ *trace the idea end-to-end*
This project is the latest step in a long decision chain; each ADR contributed a piece and a **subtlety that carries forward**:

| ADR / doc | Contributed | Subtlety carried into this project |
|---|---|---|
| **ADR-005** | Permissive licensing (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD only) | The engine + any served model + the **container image** (ffmpeg, fonts, weights) stay commercial-clean. |
| **ADR-008** | Owned-model topology; **train==serve featurizer single-sourcing** | The "core unaffected across profiles" is the same parity discipline, extended from train/serve to **local/cloud**. |
| **ADR-009** | KhelSutra Guru; **local-first delivery** | The **truly-local profile** is the direct continuation — privacy/offline self-host stays first-class. |
| **ADR-010** | DECOUPLED: DO→GCP pivot | The cloud-compute origin (GPU + co-located bucket). |
| **ADR-011** | DECOUPLED scope = M0–M2+M3.5; **deferred M3 (serving) + M4 (billing/cost-guard)**; overrode the *"rent nothing / not a service"* posture | **This project realizes M3/M4.** Carries §06's owner-gated gates: **DPA/consent for non-owner uploads, collector `md5` keying, WASB-C3**. |
| **ADR-012** | GPU-native; TPU deferred; **Cloud Run+GPU scale-to-zero = serving model**; **NVDEC** named as the decode lever | The serving substrate + the 7-step seed scope this expands. |
| **ADR-013** | Drop DO; **GCP sole compute stack**; $200 DO credits → non-compute only | The provider for cloud-serving; DO Spaces remains only a possible S3 *storage* target. |
| **→ ADR-014 (new)** | **Compute-decoupled serving:** one pure core, profile-selected compute+storage; stitch-where-metered | This doc. **Refines** ADR-011/012, does **not** supersede them. |
| PLATFORM_ARCHITECTURE.md | The `ComputeBackend`/`StorageBackend` registries | The abstraction this realizes in code. |
| VIDEO_LOCALITY_MODEL.md / DESKTOP_APP_PLAN.md | L0 fully-local tier / `LocalDesktop` backend | The truly-local profile = these, productionized. |
| 07-COMPUTE-ABSTRACTION (archived) | `DeviceContext`/`DeviceProfile` (designed) | WASB hardcodes `device='cuda'` with **no CPU fallback** — a portability gap M4 closes. |
| DEPLOY_REPORT_GCP_2026-06-20 (archived) | First real L4 run; **cross-GPU sub-pixel parity finding** | Why D5 (parity at window level, not CSV bytes). |

## 4. Design / architecture
See [`architecture.svg`](architecture.svg) for the picture. **The core never changes; the box around it does.**

### 4.1 The core contract (must stay byte/behaviour-identical) `[verified]`
1. **DETECT (GPU):** `DetectorRunner.run_predict(video_path, output_dir, video_id) → CSV[Frame,Visibility,X,Y]` (`backend/pipeline/detectors/base.py:164`). Three interchangeable impls — `WasbRunner` (WSL), `NativeWasbRunner` (Linux GPU), `StubDetectorRunner` (CPU mock) — emit the **identical CSV schema**. The trajectory CSV is the stable artifact boundary.
2. **WINDOW (CPU pure fn):** `trajectory_to_action_windows(points, fps, frame_width, …) → windows` (`tracknet_runner.py:281`). A shared `@staticmethod`, zero GPU/IO/randomness → same trajectory + config = byte-identical candidate windows. Used by every runner *and* the eval/regression stack verbatim.
3. **STITCH (CPU/ffmpeg):** `VideoCompiler.compile_highlights(raw_video, segments, out_name) → reel` (`stitcher/compiler.py:303`). Pure FS+subprocess (merge → ffprobe → watermark → per-clip libx264 trim → concat). Deterministic for a given input + ranges + watermark config.

**Non-goal (the trap to avoid):** *no* code branch inside detect/window/stitch keyed on profile — the same discipline the experiment harness already enforces (a disabled cue is byte-identical to CONTROL).

### 4.2 The deployment profiles
| Profile | Compute | Storage | Orchestration | When |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **truly-local** | `compute_target=local_gpu`; `detector_impl=native` (Linux) / `auto` (WSL); stitch **in-process** | `local` (or `gdrive` = mounted Drive) | `python -m backend.main` or `worker infer` CLI; existing async job worker | Self-host / offline / privacy / dev-on-a-GPU-box — owner (b) |
| **cloud-serving** | `compute_target=cloud_run`; `detector_impl=native` (L4, cuda); **detect AND stitch on the same Cloud Run job** | `gcs` (or gdrive mount / assembled upload); per-video workspace; reels via signed URL | Cloud Run control plane enqueues → Cloud Run GPU job pulls/runs/pushes; `WAIT_AND_RESUME` reschedules the control-plane job; scale-to-zero | Hosted SaaS — owner (a) |
| **cpu-mock** | `compute_target=cpu_mock`; `detector_impl=stub` (synth/replay) | `local`/in-memory fakes | `run_all_tests.py` / pytest | CI, regression, profile-parity, GPU-free dev |
| *byo_worker* (deferred) | tenant GPU via outbound pull agent | tenant's own bucket (signed URLs) | long-poll pull; same job table scoped by tenant | Privacy-sensitive enterprise — **DEFERRED**, enum/seam reserved |

### 4.3 Profile selection (the "startup/setup config") `[design]`
ONE new top-level `deployment` section on `AppConfig` + preset application + env auto-detect in `load_config`:
- `deployment.profile ∈ {truly-local | cloud-serving | cpu-mock}` (empty = today's manual knobs, **fully backward-compatible**).
- Selecting a profile applies a **coherent preset bundle** of *existing* knobs that today drift independently: **INPUT** (new `input_backend`/`input_root`, parallel to the output-side `StorageConfig`), **COMPUTE** (new `compute_target` enum locking `detector_impl` + `skip_ai_handoff` + workspace strategy), **STORAGE** (`storage.backend`/`workspace_root`/`single_folder_per_video`, all already exist).
- **Precedence:** built-in defaults → profile preset → `config.json` → env (`RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL`, `WASB_*`, `DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE`, …) → worker `--set k=v`. Every knob stays individually overridable.
- **Env auto-detect:** `K_SERVICE` → cloud-serving; `CI=true` → cpu-mock; else `torch.cuda.is_available()` → truly-local. **Per-profile startup checks fail/warn fast** (cloud-serving without GCS creds/GPU → loud error before any job).
- The worker (`cmd_infer`/`cmd_train`) is **already profile-agnostic** (reads `config.storage`, accepts `--config`/`--set`) → no worker rewrite.

### 4.4 Compute placement — and *why stitch runs on Cloud Run* `[design]`
DETECT on GPU everywhere; WINDOW CPU-pure everywhere; STITCH is real CPU work. In **cloud-serving, stitch is co-located in the Cloud Run job** so `gpu_active_seconds` (detect) + `wall_seconds` (detect+stitch) + `bytes_out` (reel egress) land on **one metered invocation** → the per-job cost ledger. Running stitch on a laptop would leave that cost **invisible/unattributed** — exactly what the owner wants to avoid. (Stitch stays synchronous in the GPU job for the common one-reel case; a queued CPU-only stitch fallback only if compiles get bursty — the job table + `claim_due_job` already supports both.)

### 4.5 Reuse map — most of the seams already exist `[verified]`
**Exists:** the 3-stage core; `_resolve_runner` (the single compute seam); `StubDetectorRunner` (`replay_csv` = byte-exact $0 anchor); `StorageBackend` ABC + 4 backends + `StorageRef.parse_uri`; the worker CLI (fetch/put, `--config`/`--set`); the async job control plane (`jobs` table, `claim_due_job` CAS); `ExecutionPolicy WAIT_AND_RESUME`/`JobWaiting` (carries to Cloud Run dispatch verbatim); the chunked-upload router (`<2GB`); `skip_ai_handoff` (LocalNoAI); storage fakes; golden fixtures + experiment harness. **Partial:** per-video workspace helper (default-OFF); `DeviceContext` (designed); edge-auth (v4 `user_id` columns exist). **Net-new:** the `deployment` section; `input_backend`; configurable output base (kill hardcoded `output/`); Cloud Run dispatch shim + container image; cost ledger + pricebook; edge-auth header extraction; startup env detection.

## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do / prove
- A green CI suite proves **plumbing + determinism (window+stitch) + profile-parity** for $0 — it does **NOT** prove field quality, nor that real-GPU detection is good (that's owner-side, real-video).
- **Cross-GPU detection is NOT byte-identical** — parity is a window-level claim, not a CSV-byte claim.
- The cloud `0.1.0-l4` weights are **n=2 plumbing-grade** — cloud-serving ships with **Gemini handoff ON** until the owned model clears a floor (open Q).
- This does not resolve **WASB-C3** (sharing a trained model) or the **DPA/consent** gate — both remain owner/counsel-gated before public, non-owner serving.

## 7. Deliverables & progress tracker  ⟵ **source of truth**
Legend: ☐ Todo · ◐ In progress · ☑ Done · ⛔ Gated. **One small PR per row**, off `origin/master`, no stacking. Default-OFF where it touches core callers.

| ID | Deliverable | Depends | Gated? | Status | PR |
|----|-------------|---------|--------|--------|----|
| M0 | This design doc + project dir + `runlogs/` + ADR-014 | — | owner sign-off | ◐ | this PR |
| M1 | `deployment.profile` + `compute_target` config + preset bundles + precedence/auto-detect; unit tests (no behaviour change, profile='' default) | M0 | safe | ☐ | — |
| M2 | `input_backend`/`input_root`; wire main CLI/API input through `StorageRef.parse_uri` (local=identity); storage-fake tests | M1 | safe | ☐ | — |
| M3 | Configurable output/scratch base — kill hardcoded `output/` (`trajectory_hybrid:237,313`, `yolo_hybrid:407`, `gemini`); **DEFAULT stays `output/`**; golden + stub-E2E byte-identical | M1 | ⛔ default-OFF + Launch Report on flip | ☐ | — |
| M4 | `DeviceContext` + WASB **CPU-fallback**; unified healthcheck/device wiring | M1 | safe (cuda default unchanged) | ☐ | — |
| M5 | **truly-local profile** end-to-end (preset + startup checks); first committed **runlog** (truly-local sample run) | M2,M3,M4 | owner real-GPU | ☐ | — |
| M6 | Cloud Run GPU **dispatch shim** (control plane → job via storage → re-claim) + **containerized worker image** (ffmpeg+CUDA+WASB+fonts); mocked-dispatch tests | M5 | ⛔ owner (GCP spend) | ☐ | — |
| M7 | **cloud-serving profile** e2e on L4: upload`<2GB` + gdrive/bucket → detect+stitch on Cloud Run → reel via signed URL; **DEPLOY_REPORT** (posterity, sample runs + contact sheet) | M6 | ⛔ owner (spend) | ☐ | — |
| M8 | Per-job **cost ledger** (v_ migration: `owner_id, gpu_active_seconds, wall_seconds, bytes_out, cost_usd, cost_breakdown_json`) + **pricebook** + aggregation + `/api/.../cost` | M7 | ⛔ owner (billing) | ☐ | — |
| M9 | **Edge auth** (Cf-Access extraction) + per-tenant scoping on `user_id`; DPA/consent gate wiring | M7 | ⛔ owner (privacy/counsel) | ☐ | — |
| M10 | `byo_worker` / zero-infra-BYO — **design-only, DEFERRED** | M8,M9 | ⛔ explicit owner approval | ☐ | — |

**Testing strategy is a first-class deliverable** → [`TESTING_STRATEGY.md`](TESTING_STRATEGY.md). **Run logs / sample runs for posterity** → [`runlogs/`](runlogs/).

## 8. Open questions — owner *(blocking-gates vs nice-to-knows)*
1. **Cold-start:** Cloud Run GPU job (container + CUDA + WASB conda load) cold-start vs a warm pool / `min-instances=1` — acceptable UX budget? ⛔ shapes M6/M7.
2. **Stitch placement:** always co-located in the detect job (simplest, one metered unit) vs split to a cheap CPU Cloud Run service when bursty? *Default co-located unless metrics say otherwise.*
3. **Pricebook:** source of truth (table vs config) + currency (INR vs USD, Bangalore market) + re-versioning cadence. ⛔ shapes M8.
4. **Edge auth:** Cloudflare Access vs Firebase; trust the `Cf-Access` header only behind the proxy (spoofing risk if the Cloud Run URL is hit directly). ⛔ shapes M9.
5. **DPA/consent + minors:** runtime consent gate for hosted uploads; per-tenant DPA before raw video persists on our infra (counsel). ⛔ shapes M9.
6. **Quality floor:** cloud-serving ships with Gemini handoff ON until the owned model clears a floor? (n=2 `0.1.0-l4` is plumbing-only.)
7. **gdrive in cloud:** a mounted Drive is local-FS — a Cloud Run container can't mount the tenant's Drive → is "gdrive" upload-from-Drive client-side, or truly-local-only?
8. **NVDEC/NVENC** in the Cloud Run image (cut stitch wall-time/cost) vs libx264 (determinism/parity)?
9. **Input cap:** is `<2GB` a hard client reject, or accept to the 4GB upload cap and route larger files elsewhere?
10. **Directory name:** `COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/` (chosen) vs keep `CLOUD_RUN_GPU_SERVING/` — confirm.

## 9. Definition of done
- [ ] M1–M4 land (config + input + output-base + device layer), each green on golden cross-OS + experiment no-regression.
- [ ] **Profile-parity test green** — truly-local vs cloud-mock produce byte-identical windows + segment ranges.
- [ ] **truly-local** (M5) and **cloud-serving** (M7) each have a committed **runlog / DEPLOY_REPORT** with a real sample run.
- [ ] Cost ledger (M8) makes GPU + **stitch** + egress cost visible per job; edge auth (M9) gates multi-tenant.
- [ ] → flip Status DONE, archive to `docs/archives/past_projects/`.

## 10. References
**ADR lineage:** ADR-005/008/009/010/011/012/013 → **ADR-014** (`docs/archives/decisions/DECISIONS.md`). **Strategy:** `PLATFORM_ARCHITECTURE.md`, `HOSTING_PLAN.md`, `VIDEO_LOCALITY_MODEL.md`, `DESKTOP_APP_PLAN.md`, `LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md` (khelsutra). **Archived predecessor:** `docs/archives/past_projects/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/` (esp. `05-COST-ATTRIBUTION.md`, `07-COMPUTE-ABSTRACTION.md`, `06-RISKS-AND-OPEN-QUESTIONS.md`, `DEPLOY_REPORT_GCP_2026-06-20.md`). **Code anchors `[verified]`:** `backend/pipeline/detectors/base.py:164`, `tracknet_runner.py:281`, `stitcher/compiler.py:303`, `segmenters/trajectory_hybrid.py:65,237`, `backend/storage/*`, `backend/worker/__main__.py:88`, `backend/api/{uploads,job_worker}.py`, `backend/infrastructure/database.py`. **Design provenance:** workflow `wf_56274ff0` (4 code-mappers → synthesis; 58 findings).

### 326. assistant (2026-06-21T02:45:24.915Z)

# Testing Strategy — Compute-Decoupled Rally Serving

> **Companion to** [`README.md`](README.md) (§7 tracker is the source of truth). **Status:** DRAFT (2026-06-21).
> **Thesis:** the decoupling is only *real* if we can **prove the core is unaffected across profiles** — for **$0, with no GPU/WSL/network** — and quarantine the one thing that genuinely can't be byte-deterministic (cross-GPU detection) to an owner-side real-hardware layer. Tags: `[verified]` = building block already in the tree; `[design]` = to build.

---

## 0. The two guarantees we are testing
1. **Decoupling guarantee** — the same `(input bytes + config)` produces the **same windows + the same stitched segment ranges** whether the core runs truly-local or on Cloud Run. *Provable for $0* because detection can be replaced by a deterministic `replay_csv` stub.
2. **Quality guarantee** — real WASB detection on a real GPU is *good enough at the rally level*. **Not** byte-deterministic across GPU architectures (L4 vs 2070 differ sub-pixel — 684/1195 frames, per the 2026-06-20 deploy report), so it is judged at the **window/rally level**, owner-side, on the golden corpus.

The whole strategy hangs on keeping these **separate**: CI gates #1 deterministically; the owner gates #2 on real hardware.

## 1. Test layers

### L1 — Unit (pure core) `[verified]`
- **What:** `trajectory_to_action_windows` determinism; `_merge_overlapping`; `_watermark_filter` string build; `_parse_time`; **profile preset/precedence resolution** (new); `StorageRef.parse_uri` scheme dispatch.
- **How:** pytest, no GPU/net/WSL; same-input→same-output; `abs-tol 1e-6` on floats.
- **Modes:** all (pure fns are profile-independent).

### L2 — Contract (seam ABCs) `[verified]`
- **What:** `DetectorRunner` ABC — `run_predict` CSV schema + `healthcheck` non-raising — across `Wasb`/`NativeWasb`/`Stub`; `StorageBackend` ABC roundtrip (`put → exists → stat → list → fetch → delete`) across local/s3/gcs/gdrive; the new `DeviceContext` probe contract.
- **How:** stub runner for detection; **injected fake S3/GCS clients + gdrive=`tmp_path` mount** (`tests/test_storage.py`); assert the CSV columns are identical across runners.
- **Modes:** `cpu-mock` proves the contract; local/cloud use the same code paths.

### L3 — Integration (GPU-free E2E) `[verified]`
- **What:** the full trajectory-hybrid pipeline (detect → window → persist → stitch) on the stub detector; `worker cmd_infer` fetch → segment → put with fake storage; chunked-upload assemble.
- **How:** `RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=stub` (synth **and** `replay_csv`); storage fakes; `tmp_path` workspace; assert `intervals.csv` + `report.json` + a reel are produced deterministically.
- **Modes:** `cpu-mock`, but it **exercises the cloud-serving code path** (fetch/put/dispatch) with no cloud.

### L4 — **Profile-PARITY (the load-bearing proof)** `[design]`
- **What:** the explicit decoupling guarantee — run the **same `replay_csv` golden trajectory** through (a) the in-process **truly-local** path and (b) the **worker + fake-`gcs`** (cloud-serving-mock) path, and assert **byte-identical candidate windows + identical stitched segment ranges** (and, on a fixed clip, an identical reel via ffmpeg determinism).
- **How:** one test parametrized over `{profile=truly-local, profile=cloud-serving-mock}`, fed the same replay CSV + same windowing config; **diff the window list and segment ranges exactly.** Because detection is a deterministic replay here, **any divergence is necessarily a profile/seam leak** — exactly what must never happen.
- **Modes:** truly-local vs cloud-serving (mocked). **This is the test that, if green, proves the whole design.**

### L5 — E2E real-GPU (owner-side) `[design]`
- **What:** real WASB detection on a real GPU (local 2070 → truly-local; GCP L4 → cloud-serving) end-to-end → reel; cross-GPU parity checked at the **rally/window level** (NOT CSV bytes).
- **How:** owner runs the **golden corpus** on the GPU box and on Cloud Run; compare rally counts/windows; **smoke-test the reel** (`ffmpeg -f null -` full decode exit 0 + a 5-frame contact sheet visually confirming real rallies). Recorded in a **DEPLOY_REPORT** under `runlogs/`.
- **Modes:** truly-local + cloud-serving on **real hardware**.

## 2. Real-GPU vs mocked — the split
- **Mocked is the CI default.** The stub detector (CPU, deterministic, **byte-exact via `replay_csv`**) + in-memory storage fakes prove **all plumbing + the profile-parity guarantee** with **$0** and no GPU/WSL/network.
- **Real-GPU is owner-side only** (the golden corpus + labelled videos live with the owner). It validates that `NativeWasbRunner` actually uses the GPU and that cross-GPU detection diffs are **sub-pixel** — judged at the window level, never CSV bytes.
- **The contract:** CI gates the deterministic post-trajectory transforms (window + stitch) **byte-exact**; real-GPU gates that detection on the chosen GPU is **good enough at the rally level**. Honest-limits caveat (golden §6) baked in: **green CI proves plumbing/determinism, not field quality.**

## 3. CI gates (extend the existing lanes)
Today's lanes: `ruff`, `mypy` (lenient baseline), import-smoke (no GPU stack), full suite (offline, mocked, `--cov-fail-under`), golden-fixtures cross-OS (ubuntu/windows/macos). **Add:**
- **Profile-parity lane** — the L4 test (truly-local vs cloud-mock identical windows/segments).
- **Storage-fakes roundtrip lane** — L2 across all four backends.
- **Per-PR core-touching gate** — any PR touching detect/window/stitch *callers* (esp. **M3**, the `output/` de-hardcode) must show: golden fixtures green on **all 3 OSes** AND `experiment.compare(CONTROL, profile-variant)` **F1-delta ≈ 0** (no-regression), AND **coverage not regressed** vs base.
- **No real GPU / net / WSL in CI** — ever. Real validation is the owner's L5.

## 4. Profile-parity — why it's airtight
The decoupling claim is proven by feeding one `replay_csv` golden trajectory through **both** the in-process path and the worker+fake-bucket path and asserting **byte-identical** windows + segment ranges. Detection is replaced by a deterministic replay, so **the only variable is the seam** (compute target + storage backend). If the outputs ever diverge, a profile leaked into the core — the precise failure D1 forbids. Real cross-GPU detection diffs are **quarantined** to L5 and judged at the window level.

## 5. Sample runs / runlogs for posterity (the DECOUPLED convention)
Each real run writes a dated report under [`runlogs/`](runlogs/) (e.g. `DEPLOY_REPORT_<profile>_<date>.md`), mirroring `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/DEPLOY_REPORT_GCP_2026-06-20.md`, capturing:
- resources provisioned + the **exact commands**;
- run results — **rally counts, parity numbers**, `gpu_active_seconds` / `wall_seconds` / `bytes_out`;
- a **reel smoke-test** — `ffmpeg -f null -` exit 0 + a 5-frame contact sheet visually confirming real rallies;
- gotchas (conda-ToS, WASB-C3, GPU stockouts, font fallback) + the **final teardown state** ($0 audit);
- so the next deploy is **one-shot**.

**DoD:** `truly-local` (M5) and `cloud-serving` (M7) each get **≥1 committed sample run** here.

## 6. Coverage map — what each milestone must prove before merge
| Milestone | Must be green |
|---|---|
| M1 config | L1 preset/precedence; profile='' = today (no behaviour change) |
| M2 input_backend | L2 storage roundtrip (4 backends); L3 worker fetch with local=identity |
| M3 output base (⛔ core callers) | golden cross-OS byte-identical + experiment no-regression + **L4 parity** |
| M4 DeviceContext | L2 device probe; cuda default unchanged; CPU-fallback unit-tested |
| M5 truly-local | L1–L4 all green + **owner L5 truly-local runlog** |
| M6 dispatch + image | mocked-dispatch tests; image builds; ffmpeg/fonts/CUDA on PATH |
| M7 cloud-serving | **owner L5 cloud DEPLOY_REPORT** (real L4, reel smoke-test) |
| M8 cost ledger | unit tests on the pricebook + aggregation; reconcile probes vs platform billing in the runlog |
| M9 edge auth | per-tenant scoping tests on `user_id`; header-trust/spoofing guard test |

## 7. Anti-goals / pitfalls (explicit)
- **Don't** assert detector-CSV byte-equality across GPUs — it *will* false-fail (D5).
- **Don't** add a profile `if` inside detect/window/stitch — the parity test exists to catch exactly that.
- **Don't** let `output/` de-hardcoding (M3) silently change the default layout — keep `output/` the default; Launch Report on any flip.
- **Don't** confuse truly-local's intentionally-unmetered stitch with a cost bug — only cloud-serving meters.

### 329. assistant (2026-06-21T02:45:44.393Z)

# runlogs/ — sample runs & deploy reports for posterity

This directory is the **durable record of real runs** of the compute-decoupled serving profiles —
the same convention proven by the archived `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/DEPLOY_REPORT_GCP_2026-06-20.md`. The
goal: **the next deploy is one-shot**, and anyone can trace exactly what was run, what it cost, and
what came out.

## When to add a file here
- Any **real-hardware run** (owner-side L5 in [`../TESTING_STRATEGY.md`](../TESTING_STRATEGY.md)) of a profile.
- Every **default flip** that touches the core ships its Launch Report record here too (per [[launch-report-convention]]).

## Naming
`DEPLOY_REPORT_<profile>_<YYYY-MM-DD>.md` — e.g. `DEPLOY_REPORT_truly-local_2026-07-01.md`,
`DEPLOY_REPORT_cloud-serving_2026-07-01.md`.

## Required contents (mirror the DECOUPLED deploy report)
1. **TL;DR** — what ran, profile, ✅/⏳/⛔ per stage (detect / window / stitch / serve).
2. **Resources** — exact machine/VM/Cloud-Run config, region, image; for cloud, the bucket + SA + budget.
3. **Exact commands** — copy-pasteable (`worker infer …`, the dispatch call, the config/profile used).
4. **Run results** — rally counts, **profile-parity numbers** (windows/segments vs the other profile),
   and the metering trio **`gpu_active_seconds` / `wall_seconds` / `bytes_out`** (even before the M8 ledger lands).
5. **Reel smoke-test** — `ffmpeg -f null -` full decode (exit 0) + a 5-frame contact sheet visually
   confirming real rallies (court/net/players mid-rally).
6. **Gotchas** — conda-ToS, WASB-C3, GPU stockouts, font fallback, cold-start, NVDEC, etc.
7. **Cost** — measured/estimated; reconcile probe metrics vs the platform's per-invocation billing.
8. **Teardown / final state** — for cloud, the `$0` audit (`gcloud compute instances list` + `disks list` empty); **DELETE, never just stop** ([[gcp-vm-always-delete]]).

## DoD coupling
`COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING` is not DONE until **`truly-local` (M5)** and **`cloud-serving` (M7)** each
have **≥1 committed report here** (README §9).

## Index (append as runs land)
| Date | Profile | Result | File |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | *(none yet — M5/M7 will add the first two)* | — |

  <title>Compute-Decoupled Rally Serving — architecture</title>
  <desc>One locked core engine (DETECT then WINDOW then STITCH) that never branches on deployment profile. A StorageBackend chooses where input bytes come from and outputs go; a ComputeBackend (deployment profile) chooses where the core runs. One startup config selects both. Three profiles: truly-local (local GPU, in-process stitch), cloud-serving (Cloud Run GPU runs detect and stitch, metered into a cost ledger), and cpu-mock (CI: stub plus fakes).</desc>

### 339. user (2026-06-21T02:48:46.266Z)

632	### Consequences
633	
634	- The **deferred M3 (inference serving endpoint + per-video billing) and M4 (scale-to-zero + cost
635	  guard)** from ADR-011 become a **new tracked subproject — "Cloud Run + GPU serving"** — to be spun
636	  up (as a `docs/PROJECT_DOC_TEMPLATE.md`-based doc) **AFTER `docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/` is archived**
637	  (owner sequencing). Seed scope below.
638	- **First subproject work item = containerize `python -m backend.worker`** for Cloud Run. This is the
639	  known hard part: WASB's torch-1.11 conda env makes the image non-trivial (two runtimes — the
640	  indexer venv + the `wasb` conda env). The decoupled storage/compute seams + the native runner
641	  (`detector_impl="native"`, PR #256) make the *code* portable; packaging is the gap.
642	- Keep the **storage/compute decoupling seams** intact so the SAME container runs local / GPU VM /
643	  Cloud Run (the per-video workspace convention, in progress, reinforces this).
644	- **Edge auth is a hard prerequisite** before any public serving (engine API is CORS `*`, no auth) —
645	  carried from the appliance track.
646	- **Region/capacity reality:** GPU stockouts are common (Mumbai was fully stocked-out on 2026-06-20);
647	  scale-to-zero + multi-region/flexible-zone selection is part of why Cloud Run+GPU fits.
648	- TPU is explicitly **out of scope**; this ADR records *why* so it isn't relitigated.
649	
650	#### Seed scope for the "Cloud Run + GPU serving" subproject (formalize post-archival)
651	1. Containerize `backend.worker` (indexer venv + `wasb` conda env; NVDEC-capable base image).
652	2. Cloud Run + GPU (L4) service: `infer` over a `gcs://` workspace; scale-to-zero.
653	3. Per-video cost ledger (on the `jobs` table) = the per-customer bill (M3).
654	4. Cost guard + concurrency caps (M4).
655	5. Edge auth (tenancy boundary) before public exposure.
656	6. NVDEC GPU-decode to kill the extraction bottleneck.
657	7. DPA / consent gate for non-owner uploads (productization, not compute).
658	
659	## ADR-013: Drop DigitalOcean for compute — GCP is the SOLE compute stack (training + serving)
660	
661	**Date:** 2026-06-20 · **Status:** RATIFIED (owner, in-session: "drop DO and document it… record it and bake it for all future work"). **Supersedes the DO=training split in ADR-011; refines ADR-010 + ADR-012.**
662	
663	### Decision
664	
665	1. **DigitalOcean is DROPPED as a compute provider.** **GCP is the sole compute stack** for BOTH training and serving. (ADR-011's "DO=training, GCP=serving" split is retired — GCP does both. The DECOUPLED scope decision in ADR-011 — M0–M2 + M3.5 — still stands.)
666	2. **Standing default for ALL future compute work = GCP** (a GPU VM today via `deploy/gcp/`; Cloud Run+GPU for serving per ADR-012). Do **not** re-evaluate DO/Paperspace for compute without a new ADR.
667	3. The `$200` DO credits, if used at all, go **only to non-compute** that DO bills cleanly (CPU droplets / a site host / Spaces storage / bandwidth) — **never GPU**.
668	
669	### Rationale (researched 2026-06-20, against DO's own docs)
670	
671	- **DO GPU is neither enabled on the account nor a clean free-credits path.** The DO API `/v2/sizes` returns **0 GPU sizes** (GPU Droplets not enabled); GPU Droplets are **North-America-only** (NYC2/TOR1/ATL1/RIC1/AMS3 — no India/Singapore); and DO's own docs state that for **newer customers DO charges the team's primary payment method** when GPU Droplets are created (anti-abuse) — so GPU spend hits the **card**, not the promo credit. The ADR-011 premise ("$200 credits fund DO training") does **not** hold.
672	- **Paperspace (now DO Gradient) free GPU is subscription-gated** (a paid "G*"/Growth plan; high-end A100/H100 needs the $39/mo Growth plan), and Paperspace bills **separately** from DO Droplet credits → the $200 credit doesn't fund it either.
673	- **GCP works today:** quota granted (`GPUS_ALL_REGIONS=1`), bucket + SA + `$100` budget provisioned, and M1/M2/M3.5 ran on an L4 for ~$1–2/run with no subscription. Real money, but trivial per run — and **one** portable PyTorch+CUDA stack (local ↔ GPU VM ↔ Cloud Run), no second provider to maintain.
674	
675	### Consequences
676	
677	- **Compute provisioning = `deploy/gcp/` only.** `SETUP_NEW_MACHINE.md` repointed to GCP; `deploy/digitalocean/README.md` marked **DEPRECATED**; `HOSTING_PLAN.md`'s "All-DigitalOcean" compute option retired.
678	- **`deploy/digitalocean/` scripts are KEPT** — `bootstrap.sh` / `mount_scratch.sh` / `gpu_setup.sh` are **provider-agnostic** Linux-GPU setup the GCP runbook reuses; only the DO-specific *provisioning* (doctl droplet create, Spaces) is dropped. (Optional later cleanup: move them to `deploy/common/`.)
679	- The shared DO CPU droplet **`claude-do-rally-test`** (another session's testbed) is **owner-coordinated** cleanup, separate from this decision; the freshly-rotated **DO API token** can be retired once that droplet is gone.
680	- **Storage stays bucket-abstracted** (`gcs://` primary). The `s3` backend still speaks any S3-compatible store, so **DO Spaces remains a *possible* storage target** even though DO *compute* is dropped.
681	- **Capacity reality:** GCP GPU stockouts are common (asia-south1/Mumbai was fully stocked-out 2026-06-20 → the M2 run landed an L4 in asia-southeast1/Singapore; weights still land in the asia-south1 bucket). Sweep zones/regions; scale-to-zero (ADR-012) is part of why Cloud Run+GPU fits.
682	- **DECOUPLED_COMPUTE** archives on M2 completion with a note that the DO leg was **dropped** (this ADR), not "delivered on DO".

|---|---|
| **README.md** | The thorough design — pure-core contract, the `ComputeBackend`/`StorageBackend` **profile abstraction**, the **startup-config selector**, compute placement (why stitch runs on Cloud Run = metered), reuse-vs-net-new map, **M0→M10 execution plan**, open questions, DoD, and the **ADR lineage table**. |
| **TESTING_STRATEGY.md** | The rigorous part — 5 layers, the load-bearing **profile-PARITY proof** ($0: same `replay_csv` trajectory through truly-local vs cloud-mock → byte-identical windows/segments), real-GPU owner-side E2E, CI gates, and the runlogs convention. |
| **architecture.svg** | Committed for posterity (rendered above). |
| **runlogs/** | Posterity home for deploy reports / sample runs (DoD: truly-local + cloud-serving each get ≥1). |
| **ADR-014** | The decision, **PROPOSED (owner-gated)**, with the full lineage + a forward-link from ADR-012. |

## The core idea (your requirements, encoded)
- **One pure core (DETECT→WINDOW→STITCH) that never branches on profile** — exactly your "core unaffected" constraint. Profile differences live only in config + the two registries.
- **truly-local** (local/USB + local GPU, inference **and** stitch in-process, zero cloud) and **cloud-serving** (upload<2GB / gdrive / bucket → a **Cloud Run GPU job runs detect AND stitch**, so stitch's compute is **metered** into a per-job cost ledger) are **co-equal profiles** — selected by one startup config. Plus `cpu-mock` (CI) and a reserved, deferred `byo_worker`.
- **Honest parity guarantee:** WINDOW+STITCH are byte-exact for a fixed trajectory (provable in CI for $0); DETECT is byte-exact only same-GPU → cross-GPU judged at the rally level, owner-side.

## ADR lineage wired (traceable end-to-end)
`ADR-005 → 008 → 009 → 010 → 011 → 012 → 013 → 014`, with each link's contribution + carried subtlety in both ADR-014 and the README, plus a **forward-link added to ADR-012**'s seed scope. ADR-014 **refines** ADR-011/012 (does not supersede) and builds on ADR-013 — stated precisely, no invented relations.

## What I need from you to move past M0
It's **owner-gated** — your design sign-off *is* the M0 gate before any code. The genuinely blocking open questions (README §8): **cold-start vs warm-pool**, **pricebook + currency (INR/USD)**, **edge-auth choice + header-spoofing guard**, **DPA/consent + minors**, and the **quality floor** (ship cloud with Gemini-on until the owned model clears it?). Also a quick confirm on the **directory name** (I went profile-neutral `COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING`).

Whenever you've reviewed, I can start **M1** (the `deployment.profile` config — pure, additive, default-OFF, zero core impact) as the smallest-safe first PR.

# Documentation Status & Archival Register

> **Status:** 🟢 **LIVING** — the single board for doc **lifecycle + archival due-diligence**. **Last full review:** 2026-06-21.
> **Update cadence:** whenever a doc changes lifecycle state (created / shipped a milestone / delivered / archived), and re-review at each clean-slate.
> **Why this exists:** so we can (a) **review completeness** at a glance, (b) **update it as work finishes** to prove we're done, and (c) **never archive a foundational doc without honestly updating it first** (owner directive, 2026-06-21).

---

## 0. How to use this
- **Reviewing now?** Read §4 (the open findings/actions) — that's what needs attention. §3 is the full register.
- **Marking something complete?** Update the doc's own **§7 progress tracker** (the source of truth for *its* completeness), then flip its row here, then — if terminal — run the §2 archival checklist.
- **This register does NOT duplicate completeness detail** — it indexes lifecycle + archive-readiness and points to each tracked doc's §7.

## 1. Lifecycle legend
`DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE/DELIVERED → ARCHIVED`. Plus: **LIVING** (permanent reference, never "done"), **PROPOSAL/DESIGN** (owner-gated, not started), **REPORT** (a terminal record of a run/launch), **CONVENTION** (a standing rule/guide).

## 2. Archival due-diligence checklist  ⟵ *the repeatable process (do NOT just `git mv`)*
Before a foundational/tracked doc moves to `docs/archives/`:
1. **VERIFY claims against the code** — no stale assertions; tag load-bearing facts `[verified]`. (A doc that says "nothing built" when code shipped is *not* archive-ready — fix it first.)
2. **Flip Status → DELIVERED / DONE / REJECTED + a completion note** — what shipped, where (PRs, paths, artifacts), and what was **deferred / carried forward**.
3. **Update ALL inbound references** — `docs/README.md` index, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/NEXT_STEPS.md`, and any cross-doc links → the new path + status.
4. **Keep the lineage** — ADR forward/back-links + "supersedes / superseded-by" so the evolution stays traceable.
5. **`git mv` the whole dir/file → `docs/archives/past_projects/`** (terminal-state ONLY; live docs stay at `docs/` top level). Relative links inside become historical (note this in the doc's header).
6. **Record the move** — flip the row here to §3e + a line in memory `current-state`.

> **Worked example (the template):** **DECOUPLED_COMPUTE → #270** — verified M0–M2+M3.5 delivered on GCP, flipped 🚧→✅ DELIVERED with a completion note (incl. carried-forward WASB-C3/DPA/`md5`), reindexed `docs/README` + `past_projects/README`, kept the ADR-010→014 lineage, then `git mv` to `docs/archives/past_projects/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/`.

## 3. The register

### 3a. Tracked projects — lifecycle docs (archive when DONE; each doc's §7 is the completeness source-of-truth)

| `COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/` | DRAFT (owner-gated) — created #272 | No (just started) | Owner design sign-off (M0 gate) → M1 config PR |

2	- [RALLY_QUALITY_RESEARCH.md](RALLY_QUALITY_RESEARCH.md) — **★ Foundation doc** for closing the local-vs-Gemini rally-detection gap: the measured gap + the code-grounded over-merge diagnosis ("shuttle moving ≠ rally"), an inventory of the eval/experiment harness + its gaps, the strengthened evaluation framework (segmental Edit/F1@k that make over-segmentation first-class, golden-corpus + active learning, Gemini-agreement as a *shadow* signal), an **adversarially-verified, cited external-research synthesis** (TAL/TAS boundary heads, racket-sports rally-state cues, small-data, eval rigor), a **prioritized cheap→durable roadmap with falsifiable success criteria**, and (§7) a **tracked project plan** — work-item checklist, sequencing, the single quality **number** attributed to the effort, and a **definition of done** (project completes when all tiers land + the number is recorded). The plan that the [QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md](QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md) log executes against; ties together EXPERIMENT_HARNESS / MULTI_SIGNAL_FUSION_PLAN / ANALYZERS_AND_RECONCILERS / OWNED_MODEL_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.
3	- [RALLY_DETECTION_GAP_CLOSURE.md](RALLY_DETECTION_GAP_CLOSURE.md) — **Tracked execution doc (2026-06-18)** for closing the remaining rally-detection accuracy gap **per video, before launch**, driven by the growing golden corpus. First ground-truth clip (GX010141) reframed it: boundaries are tight (R2/R3/R4 holds) — the gap is **detection**: (G1) recall (local & Gemini both miss ~half on hard clips, and *different* rallies → complementary), (G2) precision (local over-fires), (G3) **rally-END over-extension on the quick-return-after-point** (shuttle still moving → needs a *player-state* signal). Levers (owner-gated): player-kinematics + shuttle-in-hand / sustained-invisibility end-rule with reverse-timeline backtrack, `rally_gate` Gate A wiring, complementary fusion. **Peer** of `RALLY_QUALITY_RESEARCH.md` (execution tracker, not research); §7 is the source of truth.
4	- [PROMINENT_COURT_DETECTION.md](PROMINENT_COURT_DETECTION.md) — **Design + experiment (2026-06-19, default-OFF, owner-gated flip)** for the **multicourt G2 over-fire**: identify the **prominent (foreground) court** (the one covering the majority of the frame) and keep only rallies whose shuttle lives inside it — re-defining the shuttle signal `(x,y,visible)` → `(x,y,visible-AND-inside-prominent-court)` *before* windowing, so a background/adjacent-court shuttle never forms a rally. 2D floor polygon (MVP) → **pseudo-3D play-volume** (refinement, so high clears aren't clipped). Born from the GX010142 rally-#1 background-court FP; reuses `fusion_features.point_in_polygon` + `FusionConfig.court_polygon`. §8 progress tracker is the source of truth.
5	- [RALLY_DETECTION_QUALITY_REPORT.md](RALLY_DETECTION_QUALITY_REPORT.md) — **Technical-paper checkpoint (2026-06-17)** of rally-detection quality: the honest measured state (over-segmentation milestone tolerance-F1 0.091→0.323 at n=11, p=0.002; merge-rate 0.694→0.150; first real-footage ground-truth at-par with START at Gemini parity), the task-faithful R2 metric, a detailed Gemini comparison, next steps + expected wins, areas yet to be explored, and a publishability / path-to-submission verdict. Renders to a polished PDF; the externally-shareable "toward a paper" summary of `RALLY_QUALITY_RESEARCH.md` + `QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md`.
6	- [EVALUATION.md](EVALUATION.md) — How we measure and improve quality.
7	- [EXPERIMENT_HARNESS.md](EXPERIMENT_HARNESS.md) — Design (P1 SHIPPED) for A/B + shadow + SxS experimentation on rally-detection variants with **zero production regression** — experiments are decoupled from deploy (new cues default OFF, promote only on measured LOVO lift, rollback = flip a flag). Composes the existing `backend/eval/` machinery (`calibration`, `regression`, `metrics`); implemented in `backend/eval/experiment.py`.
8	- [QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md](QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md) — **Living log** of rally-detection quality iterations (hypothesis → what shipped → measured/expected effect → lesson). The reverse-chronological record; terminal-state snapshots get archived under `archives/quality-iterations/`.
9	- [ANALYZERS_AND_RECONCILERS.md](ANALYZERS_AND_RECONCILERS.md) — Design (owner-gated, NOT started): a **signal-fusion framework** for rally detection — producers emit confidence-scored **signals** at three temporal **scopes** (frame detectors / window analyzers / session analyzers), a learned **scorer** weights them, and an auditable **report-first reconciliation** pass lints the decision against the evidence. The spine: **no special-casing in the decision path** (signals→scorer, never `if/else`). Worked examples: a service-fault analyzer, a warm-up/practice span detector, and consuming a per-frame shuttle-state detector. A peer to `EXPERIMENT_HARNESS.md` and `MULTI_SIGNAL_FUSION_PLAN.md` (ADR-007 modular rally layer).
10	- [PERSONALIZATION_PLAN.md](PERSONALIZATION_PLAN.md) — Design (owner-adopted **hybrid**; Phase-0 landing) for the **per-user personalization feature on a generalization-first baseline**: a thin per-user *tuning* layer gated by the honest small-N statistics (a **min-N promotion floor** + a **structural-guard exemption**, never a service gate) + a **contribution flywheel** (free-tier videos pool into the owner-trained baseline; *"a tool that gets better the more you help it"*). Records the **locked owner decisions** (identity, storage, `min_n` semantics, Gate-A default, free-tier pooling). Built so far: per-user promotion gate (#141) + held-out-USER learning curve (#142).
11	
12	- [GOLDEN_REGRESSION_FIXTURES.md](GOLDEN_REGRESSION_FIXTURES.md) — **Design (owner-gated, NOT started):** video-free, non-attributable regression fixtures so a clean clone runs the rally-seg suite with **no video/GPU/DB**. ---
name: doc-status-register
description: docs/DOC_STATUS.md is the living doc-lifecycle register; archive foundational docs only AFTER honestly updating them (verify → flip status+completion note → update refs → keep lineage → move).
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

Owner directive (2026-06-21): as the repo matures, do **due diligence on docs** — keep a reviewable completeness register, and **archive a foundational doc only AFTER honestly updating it**, never a bare `git mv`.

**Why:** docs drift — claims go stale, projects finish without a status flip, and duplicates accumulate (found 2026-06-21: `PER_VIDEO_OUTPUT_LAYOUT.md` [design, 2026-06-14] is superseded by `PER_VIDEO_WORKSPACE.md` [in-progress]; `NEXT_STEPS.md` is 8 days stale). A single living register + a repeatable archival checklist keeps the strategy tree honest and reviewable.

**How to apply:**
- **`docs/DOC_STATUS.md`** is the **living register** — every foundational/tracked doc's lifecycle + archive-readiness + open doc-hygiene findings (§4). Update a row when a doc changes state; re-review §4 each clean-slate. Linked from `docs/README.md` (top) + the `CLAUDE.md` archive policy.
- **Archival due-diligence checklist (DOC_STATUS §2)** — before moving a doc to `docs/archives/`: (1) **verify claims vs code** (no stale assertions); (2) **flip Status → DELIVERED/DONE/REJECTED + a completion note** (what shipped / where / deferred); (3) **update ALL inbound refs** (`docs/README`, `CLAUDE.md`, `NEXT_STEPS`, cross-links); (4) **keep the lineage** (ADR + supersedes/superseded-by); (5) **`git mv` the whole dir → `docs/archives/past_projects/`** (terminal-state ONLY); (6) record in `current-state`.
- **Worked example / template:** DECOUPLED_COMPUTE archival (#270). Pairs with [[working-agreement-tracked-project-docs]]; the [[launch-report-convention]] is the analogous discipline for default flips.

### 393. user (2026-06-21T03:22:08.256Z)

22	output/
23	  <video_slug>/                 # slug = sanitized stem + short video_id (collision-safe)
24	    runlog.txt                  # the per-video run log (today: output/<video>_run.log)
25	    sports_indexer.db           # per-video DB (or a row-scoped view of a shared DB — see below)
26	    wasb/<key>_wasb.csv         # the trajectory (today: output/wasb/)
27	    handoff/                    # AI-handoff clips (today: output/chunks_*_handoff/, auto-cleaned)
28	    rallies.csv                 # detected rally [start,end] + shot_count
29	    segments/                   # per-segment clips / pngs / debug frames
30	    highlights.mp4              # the reel
31	    report.json / report.md     # the observability report
32	```
33	A directory with 5 videos → 5 subdirectories, each holding **everything** for that video.
34	
35	## Design
36	- **`video_slug(video_path, video_id) -> str`** — a new helper (next to `utils/hashing.py`):
37	  sanitized stem + `video_id[:12]` (the MD5 already used as the cache key), e.g.
38	  `GX010125_1080p__89605e80ed01`. Collision-safe (same stem / different bytes → different dir).
39	- **Thread one `run_dir` through the pipeline** instead of the literal `"output"`:
40	  - `TrajectoryHybridSegmenter.process_video` → `out_dir = os.path.join(run_dir, self.family)`
41	    and the handoff dir under `run_dir` (replaces the two hardcoded `"output"` joins).
42	  - The `wasb_runner`/`tracknet_runner` already take `output_win_dir` — just pass the per-video one.
43	  - The CLI `--output-dir` (already in `backend/main.py`) becomes the **parent**; the pipeline
44	    creates `<output-dir>/<video_slug>/` and writes everything under it.
45	- **DB choice (owner decision):** (a) a per-video `sports_indexer.db` in the slug dir (simplest,
46	  fully isolated, but loses cross-video queries), or (b) keep the shared DB and only isolate
47	  *files* (the slug dir holds artifacts; the DB stays one queryable store keyed by `video_id`).
48	  **Recommend (b)** — the DB is already `video_id`-scoped; isolate the heavy *file* artifacts,
49	  keep one queryable metadata store. The slug dir then omits its own DB.
50	- **Back-compat:** default parent stays `output/`; a flag (or just the default-on behaviour)
51	  switches flat→per-video. Old flat outputs are left untouched; new runs go to slug dirs.
52	
53	## Phasing (owner-gated; ONE PR per slice, off `master`)
54	- **L0 — this plan.**
55	- **L1 — `video_slug` helper + thread `run_dir`** through `trajectory_hybrid` (replace the two
56	  hardcoded `"output"` joins) + the per-video subdir creation; CLI `--output-dir` = parent.
57	  Pure plumbing; a regression test pinning that all artifacts land under the slug dir.
58	- **L2 — runlog + report + rallies.csv into the slug dir**; migrate the eval/CSV writers.
59	- **L3 — cache-hygiene + cleanup_caches awareness** of the new layout (per-video purge).
60	
61	## Relation to the first isolated run
62	`output/GX010125_run/` (the 2026-06-14 offline re-score) is the **manual prototype** of this
63	layout — it isolated the rally CSVs + summary, but the trajectory still lives in the shared
64	`output/wasb/` precisely because of the hardcoded path L1 fixes. This plan makes that isolation
65	automatic and complete for every video.
66

### 397. user (2026-06-21T03:23:18.596Z)

1	# Per-video workspace — one folder per video, local⇄cloud identical
2	
3	> **Status:** `DRAFT` — `in progress` (owner-requested 2026-06-20) · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-06-20 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-20
4	> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
5	> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; pointer in memory `current-state`.
6	> **Relation to existing docs:** peer-of `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE` (reinforces the storage/compute seam) and the appliance track; extends `file-placement-conventions`.
7	> **Honesty note:** claims tagged `[verified]` (checked vs code by the mapping workflow), `[design]` (proposed), `[researched]`.
8	
9	---
10	
11	## 0. TL;DR
12	Today a single video's artifacts are **scattered** — `output/<family>/*.csv`, `output/chunks_<provider>_handoff/`, `~/clips/<key>_frames/`, `<scratch>/outputs/<video_id>/`, reels in `output/`, logs to stdout, plus DB rows. The mapping workflow found **56 write sites across 4 subsystems** `[verified]`. Goal: **one folder per video, keyed by the MD5 `video_id`, holding everything about that video** (working video copy/pointer, frames, trajectory, candidates, intervals/report, reel, run log) **except its metadata, which stays in the DB**. The root is a **storage URI**, so a **local** deployment and a **cloud (bucket)** deployment use the *same relative layout* — only the root differs → easy to find, easy to repro, easy to clean. Single most important caveat: this is a **default-OFF, phased** convergence (legacy flat layout untouched until a Launch-Report flip) because the worker's bucket push prefix and the WSL/native fast-scratch paths are load-bearing.

### 435. assistant (2026-06-21T04:41:17.563Z)

# data_pipeline/ — data collection · annotation · golden sets · sharing  (**NO ML**)

The **data layer**: everything about *getting, recording, annotating, assembling, and sharing/ingesting
the rally **data*** — deliberately **isolated from the ML work**. Segmentation, stitching, models, and
the model-eval framework live at `docs/` top level + `COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/`, **not here**.

**In scope (what belongs here):**
- [`VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES.md`](VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES.md) — how to capture footage (collection).
- [`GOLDEN_SET_IMPLEMENTATION.md`](GOLDEN_SET_IMPLEMENTATION.md) · [`GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md`](GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md) · [`GOLDEN_VIDEOS.md`](GOLDEN_VIDEOS.md) — golden-set creation + which videos.
- [`GOLDEN_DATA_SHARING.md`](GOLDEN_DATA_SHARING.md) — how the data/artifacts are shared + ingested (collector / OneDrive flow).

**Deliberately NOT here:**
- **ML / model work** — segmentation + stitching design, the rally-quality eval framework (`../EVALUATION.md`, `../RALLY_QUALITY_RESEARCH.md`, the R-series), and the regression-fixtures harness (`../GOLDEN_REGRESSION_FIXTURES.md`, which is ML-eval). Where a doc here references the ML side, it is a **pointer** (`../X.md`), not ML detail.
- **The annotation *vendor* engagement** — the Roora docs (`ROORA_LABELING_GUIDELINES`, `GOLDEN_SET_VENDORING`) are archived under [`../archives/roora-vendor/`](../archives/roora-vendor/).

> Boundary established 2026-06-21 (owner). The data pillar is reusable knowledge about the *inputs*; the ML pillar is the *processing*. Keep them separate.

### 485. assistant (2026-06-21T06:36:55.464Z)

Q1–Q3 locked ✅ (scale-to-zero · DB pricebook USD-internal/INR-display · Cloudflare Access). On Q4 — your instinct is good, and here are my honest thoughts:

**The trade is the *right* model.** "Free reels in exchange for training rights" is exactly the consent-based, commercial-clean way to grow the **owned** corpus — it *is* the "contribution flywheel" already in `PERSONALIZATION_PLAN.md` ("a tool that gets better the more you help it"), and it keeps us inside the MIT/Apache/**owned-data-only** guardrail (we never scrape; users grant it). **Yes, an explicit ToS helps** — it's the legal basis (data-for-service), and the clean shape is **free tier = grants training rights; paid tier = no-train (privacy becomes the paid feature)**.

**But two refinements are load-bearing — one is a real blocker for the academy beachhead:**
1. **Minors.** Bangalore academies are full of juniors. A coach clicking a ToS **cannot** grant training rights over a *minor's* image/data — India's DPDP Act + GDPR require **verifiable parental consent** for under-18s. So the ToS trade must be **adults-only into the training pool**: minor-containing footage still gets the free reel, but is **excluded from training** (this is already our standing guardrail — the ToS operationalizes it via an upload-time attestation). This needs **counsel review** before any public free tier.
2. **Train on *derived* data, not raw pixels.** The rally model (Gen-0/1) trains on **trajectories + rally labels**, not video pixels — so we can train on the derived signal and **auto-delete the raw video** after derivation. Much smaller privacy footprint, and the rally trajectory isn't biometric (person-detection stays off; gait/biometrics are future-gated). Gen-2 VLM (pixels) is separate + deferred.

**Timing:** lock the *direction* now; the live "training-on-uploads" switch is gated to **M9 (public signup) behind counsel** — it doesn't block M0/M1, and truly-local needs no DPA at all. So no runway cost to deciding it now.

Two to close it out:

You can now continue with these answers in mind.

| **D7 Cold-start** | **Scale-to-zero** (accept ~1–3 min; async job; no warm pool) |
| **D8 Pricebook** | Versioned **DB table** · cost in **USD** (GCP) · displayed **INR** at a config FX |
| **D9 Edge auth** | **Cloudflare Access** (reuse the site's CF; verify Cf-Access JWT + lock ingress) |
| **D10 Data trade** | Free-tier **data-for-reels** (training rights ⇄ free reels; paid = no-train) — **adults-only** pool, **derived-data** + auto-delete raw, **counsel-reviewed ToS**, live training gated to M9 |
| **D11 Quality floor** | **Gemini-ON** until the owned model clears the #172 floor → Launch-Report flip; owned model in shadow |
| **D12 / M11** | **Data-flywheel harness** (your add): capture→store→shadow-eval→**goldenize** consented adult jobs → owned model climbs to the floor |

Your data-for-reels insight + the flywheel harness are the strongest part — they turn the free tier into the legal, consent-based engine that grows the **owned** corpus until you can switch Gemini off. The one caveat I baked in as a hard carve-out: **academy minors → reel yes, training pool no** (DPDP/GDPR need verifiable parental consent), with the ToS counsel-reviewed before public launch.

- **(c) Consent over surprise** — fully agree, and important for *cost*: **D15** makes auto-detect a *suggestion the user confirms*, and **cloud spend is never silent**.
